# Hand-written data of tools/source-1.0.2/english/rekey.py (fix 25: English keys).
#
# Format: "[section]" lines, then "<Portuguese key> = <text>" lines. As in the language files,
# "\s" is a space (needed at the start or end of a text), "\n" a new line, "\\" a backslash;
# spaces inside a text may be written as they are. Lines starting with "#" are comments.
# printf specifiers (%d, %08X) and edge spaces must be kept: the texts are joined with data.
#
# [english]  English for Portuguese keys that have English nowhere else: not in en.txt (the
#            official package's, merged with the fork's overrides), not in Tr's built-in table,
#            not in a FramegenText or CoreUiText English. Until fix 25 these showed in
#            Portuguese to an English user (and to any language whose file lacked them).
# [keys]     The English key of a Portuguese key when it is not just its English: the context
#            keys of collisions. Two Portuguese keys with the same English but different
#            translations (gender, number, or a different word) cannot share one English key,
#            so one keeps the plain English and the others get "<English>||<context>". Only the
#            part before "||" is shown when no language file has the key. rekey.py fails on any
#            collision not resolved here.
# [<code>]   Translations, for a language code (es, es-419, pt, de, fr, it, ja, ko, nl, pl, ru, tr,
#            zh-Hans, zh-Hant), of keys that are in no language file: fix 22's "Rede" row and
#            the guide's "opção"/"opções" counter. They follow each file's own words (Opções,
#            Rede desligada, the jogo/jogos counter: Polish and Russian use the plural form for
#            five and more, Japanese and Korean a counter, Turkish no plural after a number).
# [added <code>]  Translations, for a language code (pt-BR and the others, not en: the key is
#            the English), of English keys that fixes after fix 25 add: "<English key> =
#            <translation>". Fixes after fix 25 are written against English keys (Tr("...") in
#            English); tools/check-english-source.py fails until pt-BR and the 13 full files
#            translate each new key here.

# Fix 119: the Kinect colour camera from the phone (camera_picture.hpp).
Camera picture for the game = Imagem da câmera para o jogo
With a phone as the Kinect, the phone can also send a small picture from its camera for games that show the camera (replays, photos). It goes only to this console, and the emulator keeps it in memory only, never on a drive; a game may still keep photos of its own in its saves. It also needs Camera picture for the game on the phone page. Off, games see a grey picture. = Com um celular como Kinect, o celular também pode enviar uma imagem pequena da câmera para jogos que mostram a câmera (replays, fotos). Ela vai só para este console, e o emulador a mantém só na memória, nunca em uma unidade; um jogo ainda pode guardar fotos próprias nos seus saves. Também é preciso ativar Imagem da câmera para o jogo na página do celular. Desligada, os jogos veem uma imagem cinza.
Resolution scale (test) = Escala de renderização (teste)
Draws the game at twice its own resolution for a sharper picture. Uses much more video memory and graphics power; some effects (glow, shadows, pictures the game reads back) may look wrong. A test: if the game closes or looks broken, go back to 1x. = Desenha o jogo com o dobro da sua resolução, para uma imagem mais nítida. Usa muito mais memória de vídeo e processamento gráfico; alguns efeitos (brilho, sombras, imagens que o jogo lê de volta) podem ficar errados. É um teste: se o jogo fechar ou ficar com defeitos, volte para 1x.
Resolution scale (test) 2x: this game needs more memory than is left. The picture may break; set it to 1x in this game's settings. = Escala de renderização (teste) 2x: este jogo precisa de mais memória do que a que resta. A imagem pode falhar; defina 1x nas configurações deste jogo.

# Fix 137: anti-aliasing (FXAA) in the Vulkan swap (launcher option antialiasing).
Anti-aliasing = Antisserrilhamento
Smooths the jagged edges of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. FXAA: light, keeps text sharp. FXAA Extreme: smoother edges, a slightly softer picture. Vulkan only; it costs little. SMAA 1x: cleaner edges than FXAA with less blur, a little heavier. = Suaviza as bordas serrilhadas da imagem do jogo antes de ela ser ajustada à tela. FXAA: leve, mantém o texto nítido. FXAA Extremo: bordas mais suaves, imagem um pouco mais suave. Só no Vulkan; custa pouco. SMAA 1x: bordas mais limpas que o FXAA, com menos desfoque, um pouco mais pesado.
# Fix 145: SMAA 1x, the fourth anti-aliasing choice.
SMAA 1x = SMAA 1x
FXAA = FXAA
FXAA Extreme = FXAA Extremo
Anti-aliasing works with the Vulkan renderer only. = O antisserrilhamento funciona apenas com o renderizador Vulkan.
# Fix 141: the colour preset and the overscan crop in the Vulkan swap (color_preset, overscan_crop).
Color preset = Predefinição de cores
Changes the colors of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. OLED Deep Black: deeper blacks, shadow detail kept. Vibrant: richer colors. Clean Neutral: a slightly brighter, clearer picture. Vulkan only. = Muda as cores da imagem do jogo antes de ela ser ajustada à tela. OLED preto profundo: pretos mais profundos, mantendo os detalhes das sombras. Vívido: cores mais ricas. Neutro limpo: imagem um pouco mais clara e nítida. Só no Vulkan.
OLED Deep Black = OLED preto profundo
Vibrant = Vívido
Clean Neutral = Neutro limpo
Overscan crop = Corte de overscan
Cuts a thin border off each edge of the game's picture and enlarges the rest, keeping its proportions: for games that show a stripe or garbage at the edges. Vulkan only. = Corta uma borda fina de cada lado da imagem do jogo e amplia o resto, mantendo as proporções: para jogos que mostram uma faixa ou lixo nas bordas. Só no Vulkan.
Color presets and overscan crop work with the Vulkan renderer only. = As predefinições de cores e o corte de overscan funcionam apenas com o renderizador Vulkan.
900p (1600x900) = 900p (1600x900)
The resolution the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game. At 480p or 540p some games draw fewer pixels and run lighter; at 900p or 1080p some draw more (sharper, heavier). Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = A resolução que o Xbox 360 emulado informa ao jogo. Em 480p ou 540p alguns jogos desenham menos pontos e ficam mais leves; em 900p ou 1080p alguns desenham mais (mais nítido, mais pesado). Muitos jogos ignoram e continuam em 720p.
Frame rate limit (screen) = Limite de quadros (tela)
Shows the game's new pictures at most this many times a second, each for the same time, for an even motion on screen. The game itself runs as before (its speed, its sound and the Emulated video rate do not change), and a game slower than the limit is shown as it is. 40 needs the 120 Hz output (Frame generation output: Up to 120 Hz); at 60 Hz it shows every picture. It does nothing while frame generation is on. Automatic: as before. = Mostra as imagens novas do jogo no máximo esta quantidade de vezes por segundo, cada uma pelo mesmo tempo, para um movimento uniforme na tela. O jogo continua rodando como antes (a velocidade, o som e o Ritmo do vídeo emulado não mudam), e um jogo mais lento que o limite é mostrado como está. 40 precisa da saída de 120 Hz (Saída de vídeo da geração: Até 120 Hz); em 60 Hz mostra todas as imagens. Não faz nada com a geração de quadros ligada. Automático: como antes.
No limit = Sem limite

Picture and smoothness = Imagem e fluidez
Emulation (advanced) = Emulação (avançado)
Emulated video rate (game clock, not your TV) = Ritmo do vídeo emulado (relógio do jogo, não da TV)
The emulated Xbox 360's own clock: how many times a second it tells the game a new frame is due. It is not your TV's refresh rate (that is Frame generation output, under Picture and smoothness). At 120 Hz some games held at 30 frames go to 60, but others run too fast. A test, game by game. = O relógio do próprio Xbox 360 emulado: quantas vezes por segundo ele avisa o jogo de que está na hora de um novo quadro. Não é a taxa de atualização da sua TV (essa é a Saída de vídeo da geração, em Imagem e fluidez). Em 120 Hz alguns jogos travados em 30 quadros vão para 60, mas outros ficam rápidos demais. Um teste, jogo a jogo.
VSync (emulated, game speed) = VSync (emulado, velocidade do jogo)
The emulated console's vertical sync, not your TV's. On: games show at most 60 frames a second, so games with a 60 FPS patch do not speed up. Off: no cap, and some games run too fast. = A sincronização vertical do console emulado, não a da sua TV. Ligado: os jogos mostram no máximo 60 quadros por segundo, então jogos com patch de 60 FPS não aceleram. Desligado: sem limite, e alguns jogos ficam rápidos demais.
How the game's picture looks and how smoothly it is shown: image filter, anti-aliasing, colors, frame rate limit, frame generation and the rate sent to your TV. The game itself runs the same. = Como a imagem do jogo aparece e com que fluidez é mostrada: filtro de imagem, suavização de bordas, cores, limite de quadros, geração de quadros e a taxa enviada à sua TV. O jogo em si roda igual.
How the emulated Xbox 360 runs each game: its video rate and resolution, VSync, the renderer, memory, readbacks, shaders and tests. They can change a game's speed or correctness; change them game by game. = Como o Xbox 360 emulado roda cada jogo: ritmo do vídeo e resolução, VSync, renderizador, memória, leituras de volta, shaders e testes. Podem mudar a velocidade ou a correção de um jogo; mude-as jogo a jogo.
Puts every option of the Picture and smoothness, Audio, Controls, System and Emulation (advanced) pages back to its recommended value. Each game's own settings stay as they are. = Volta cada opção das páginas Imagem e fluidez, Áudio, Controles, Sistema e Emulação (avançado) ao valor recomendado. As configurações próprias de cada jogo continuam como estão.

These options change how the game runs. = Estas opções mudam como o jogo roda.
Changed for this game. Too fast or wrong? Set it back. = Mudado para este jogo. Rápido demais ou com erros? Volte o valor.
Changes how the game runs: it can run faster, slower or wrong. = Muda como o jogo roda: pode ficar mais rápido, mais lento ou com erros.
How the picture is scaled to your screen. CAS sharpens; FSR upscales with cleaner edges. = Como a imagem é ajustada à tela. CAS deixa mais nítido; FSR amplia com bordas mais limpas.
Smooths jagged edges. FXAA is light; SMAA 1x gives the cleanest edges. = Suaviza bordas serrilhadas. FXAA é leve; SMAA 1x dá as bordas mais limpas.
Changes the picture's colors: deeper blacks, richer or brighter colors. = Muda as cores da imagem: pretos mais profundos, cores mais vivas ou mais claras.
Cuts a thin border off the edges, for games with a stripe at the edge. = Corta uma borda fina das laterais, para jogos com uma faixa na borda.
How strongly CAS and FSR sharpen the picture. = Quanto o CAS e o FSR deixam a imagem nítida.
Hides banding in skies and shadows. = Esconde faixas em céus e sombras.
Keep the game's proportions, or stretch the picture to fill the screen. = Manter as proporções do jogo ou esticar a imagem para preencher a tela.
Keeps textures on floors and walls sharp at an angle. = Mantém nítidas as texturas de chãos e paredes vistos de lado.
Shows new pictures at most this often, for even motion. The game runs as before. = Mostra imagens novas no máximo nesta frequência, para um movimento uniforme. O jogo roda como antes.
The emulated console's clock, not your TV. 120 Hz can make games run too fast. = O relógio do console emulado, não da TV. 120 Hz pode deixar jogos rápidos demais.
Lighter tracking of data the game rewrites all the time. Off only to compare. = Acompanhamento mais leve dos dados que o jogo reescreve o tempo todo. Desligue só para comparar.
How much memory is sent to video again after the game writes to it. = Quanta memória volta ao vídeo depois que o jogo escreve nela.
Lets the game read back what it drew. Some effects need it; it is slower. = Deixa o jogo ler de volta o que desenhou. Alguns efeitos precisam; é mais lento.
Lets the game read data made by video. Fixes missing objects; it is slower. = Deixa o jogo ler dados feitos pelo vídeo. Corrige objetos faltando; é mais lento.
Prepares new effects in the background: fewer stops, effects may appear late. = Prepara efeitos novos em segundo plano: menos travadas, efeitos podem aparecer depois.
A fix for Team Ninja games where characters disappear. It is slower. = Correção para jogos da Team Ninja em que personagens somem. É mais lento.
Draws from memory the game already gave back, so nothing goes missing. = Desenha a partir de memória que o jogo já liberou, para nada sumir.

%s: its install data is incomplete (%s: %s of %s). In the library press Cross on the game, open the %s tab, select each of its install packages and press Triangle (%s). Na biblioteca, pressione X no jogo, abra a aba "%s", selecione cada pacote de instalação e pressione Triângulo "%s". Depois inicie o jogo para instalar de novo.
The video mode the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game; it is not your TV's resolution. It applies when the game starts. At 1080p or 900p some games draw more pixels (sharper, heavier); at 480p some draw fewer and run lighter. 540p is not a mode a real console reports: the game is told 720p. Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = O modo de vídeo que o Xbox 360 emulado informa ao jogo; não é a resolução da sua TV. Vale a partir do início do jogo. Em 1080p ou 900p alguns jogos desenham mais pixels (mais nítido, mais pesado); em 480p alguns desenham menos e ficam mais leves. 540p não é um modo de um console de verdade: o jogo recebe 720p. Muitos jogos ignoram isso e ficam em 720p.

# Fix 119: the Kinect colour camera from the phone (camera_picture.hpp).
Camera picture for the game = Imagem da câmera para o jogo
With a phone as the Kinect, the phone can also send a small picture from its camera for games that show the camera (replays, photos). It goes only to this console, and the emulator keeps it in memory only, never on a drive; a game may still keep photos of its own in its saves. It also needs Camera picture for the game on the phone page. Off, games see a grey picture. = Com um celular como Kinect, o celular também pode enviar uma imagem pequena da câmera para jogos que mostram a câmera (replays, fotos). Ela vai só para este console, e o emulador a mantém só na memória, nunca em uma unidade; um jogo ainda pode guardar fotos próprias nos seus saves. Também é preciso ativar Imagem da câmera para o jogo na página do celular. Desligada, os jogos veem uma imagem cinza.
Resolution scale (test) = Escala de renderização (teste)
Draws the game at twice its own resolution for a sharper picture. Uses much more video memory and graphics power; some effects (glow, shadows, pictures the game reads back) may look wrong. A test: if the game closes or looks broken, go back to 1x. = Desenha o jogo com o dobro da sua resolução, para uma imagem mais nítida. Usa muito mais memória de vídeo e capacidade gráfica; alguns efeitos (brilho, sombras, imagens que o jogo volta a ler) podem ficar errados. É um teste: se o jogo fechar ou ficar com defeitos, volte a 1x.
Resolution scale (test) 2x: this game needs more memory than is left. The picture may break; set it to 1x in this game's settings. = Escala de renderização (teste) 2x: este jogo precisa de mais memória do que a que resta. A imagem pode falhar; defina 1x nas definições deste jogo.

Body type = Tipo de corpo
Female = Feminino

The avatar's build: a female body has slightly wider hips and narrower shoulders. = A constituição do avatar: um corpo feminino tem ancas um pouco mais largas e ombros mais estreitos.

# Fix 137: anti-aliasing (FXAA) in the Vulkan swap (launcher option antialiasing).
Anti-aliasing = Suavização de arestas
Smooths the jagged edges of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. FXAA: light, keeps text sharp. FXAA Extreme: smoother edges, a slightly softer picture. Vulkan only; it costs little. SMAA 1x: cleaner edges than FXAA with less blur, a little heavier. = Suaviza as arestas serrilhadas da imagem do jogo antes de esta ser ajustada ao ecrã. FXAA: leve, mantém o texto nítido. FXAA Extremo: arestas mais suaves, imagem um pouco mais suave. Só em Vulkan; custa pouco. SMAA 1x: arestas mais limpas que o FXAA, com menos desfoque, um pouco mais pesado.
# Fix 145: SMAA 1x, the fourth anti-aliasing choice.
SMAA 1x = SMAA 1x
FXAA = FXAA
FXAA Extreme = FXAA Extremo
Anti-aliasing works with the Vulkan renderer only. = A suavização de arestas só funciona com o renderizador Vulkan.
# Fix 141: the colour preset and the overscan crop in the Vulkan swap (color_preset, overscan_crop).
Color preset = Predefinição de cores
Changes the colors of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. OLED Deep Black: deeper blacks, shadow detail kept. Vibrant: richer colors. Clean Neutral: a slightly brighter, clearer picture. Vulkan only. = Muda as cores da imagem do jogo antes de esta ser ajustada ao ecrã. OLED preto profundo: pretos mais profundos, mantendo os detalhes das sombras. Vívido: cores mais ricas. Neutro limpo: imagem um pouco mais clara e nítida. Só em Vulkan.
OLED Deep Black = OLED preto profundo
Vibrant = Vívido
Clean Neutral = Neutro limpo
Overscan crop = Corte de overscan
Cuts a thin border off each edge of the game's picture and enlarges the rest, keeping its proportions: for games that show a stripe or garbage at the edges. Vulkan only. = Corta uma margem fina de cada lado da imagem do jogo e amplia o resto, mantendo as proporções: para jogos que mostram uma faixa ou lixo nas margens. Só em Vulkan.
Color presets and overscan crop work with the Vulkan renderer only. = As predefinições de cores e o corte de overscan só funcionam com o renderizador Vulkan.
900p (1600x900) = 900p (1600x900)
The resolution the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game. At 480p or 540p some games draw fewer pixels and run lighter; at 900p or 1080p some draw more (sharper, heavier). Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = A resolução que o Xbox 360 emulado informa ao jogo. Em 480p ou 540p alguns jogos desenham menos pontos e ficam mais leves; em 900p ou 1080p alguns desenham mais (mais nítido, mais pesado). Muitos jogos ignoram e continuam em 720p.
Frame rate limit (screen) = Limite de fotogramas (ecrã)
Shows the game's new pictures at most this many times a second, each for the same time, for an even motion on screen. The game itself runs as before (its speed, its sound and the Emulated video rate do not change), and a game slower than the limit is shown as it is. 40 needs the 120 Hz output (Frame generation output: Up to 120 Hz); at 60 Hz it shows every picture. It does nothing while frame generation is on. Automatic: as before. = Mostra as imagens novas do jogo no máximo este número de vezes por segundo, cada uma durante o mesmo tempo, para um movimento uniforme no ecrã. O jogo continua a correr como antes (a velocidade, o som e o Ritmo do vídeo emulado não mudam), e um jogo mais lento do que o limite é mostrado tal como está. 40 precisa da saída de 120 Hz (Saída de vídeo da geração de fotogramas: Até 120 Hz); a 60 Hz mostra todas as imagens. Não faz nada com a geração de fotogramas ligada. Automático: como antes.
No limit = Sem limite

Picture and smoothness = Imagem e fluidez
Emulation (advanced) = Emulação (avançado)
Emulated video rate (game clock, not your TV) = Ritmo do vídeo emulado (relógio do jogo, não do televisor)
The emulated Xbox 360's own clock: how many times a second it tells the game a new frame is due. It is not your TV's refresh rate (that is Frame generation output, under Picture and smoothness). At 120 Hz some games held at 30 frames go to 60, but others run too fast. A test, game by game. = O relógio do próprio Xbox 360 emulado: quantas vezes por segundo avisa o jogo de que é hora de uma nova imagem. Não é a frequência de atualização do televisor (essa é a Saída de vídeo da geração de fotogramas, em Imagem e fluidez). A 120 Hz alguns jogos presos a 30 fotogramas passam a 60, mas outros correm demasiado depressa. Um teste, jogo a jogo.
VSync (emulated, game speed) = VSync (emulado, velocidade do jogo)
The emulated console's vertical sync, not your TV's. On: games show at most 60 frames a second, so games with a 60 FPS patch do not speed up. Off: no cap, and some games run too fast. = A sincronização vertical da consola emulada, não a do televisor. Ligado: os jogos mostram no máximo 60 fotogramas por segundo, por isso jogos com patch de 60 FPS não aceleram. Desligado: sem limite, e alguns jogos correm demasiado depressa.
How the game's picture looks and how smoothly it is shown: image filter, anti-aliasing, colors, frame rate limit, frame generation and the rate sent to your TV. The game itself runs the same. = Como a imagem do jogo aparece e com que fluidez é mostrada: filtro de imagem, suavização de arestas, cores, limite de fotogramas, geração de fotogramas e a frequência enviada ao televisor. O jogo em si corre igual.
How the emulated Xbox 360 runs each game: its video rate and resolution, VSync, the renderer, memory, readbacks, shaders and tests. They can change a game's speed or correctness; change them game by game. = Como o Xbox 360 emulado corre cada jogo: ritmo do vídeo e resolução, VSync, renderizador, memória, leituras de volta, shaders e testes. Podem mudar a velocidade ou a correção de um jogo; altere-as jogo a jogo.
Puts every option of the Picture and smoothness, Audio, Controls, System and Emulation (advanced) pages back to its recommended value. Each game's own settings stay as they are. = Repõe cada opção das páginas Imagem e fluidez, Áudio, Comandos, Sistema e Emulação (avançado) no valor recomendado. As definições próprias de cada jogo ficam como estão.

Changes how the game runs: it can run faster, slower or wrong. = Muda a forma como o jogo corre: pode ficar mais rápido, mais lento ou com erros.
How the picture is scaled to your screen. CAS sharpens; FSR upscales with cleaner edges. = Como a imagem é ajustada ao ecrã. CAS torna-a mais nítida; FSR amplia com arestas mais limpas.
Smooths jagged edges. FXAA is light; SMAA 1x gives the cleanest edges. = Suaviza arestas serrilhadas. FXAA é leve; SMAA 1x dá as arestas mais limpas.

Cuts a thin border off the edges, for games with a stripe at the edge. = Corta uma borda fina dos lados, para jogos com uma faixa na borda.

Hides banding in skies and shadows. = Esconde faixas em céus e sombras.
Keep the game's proportions, or stretch the picture to fill the screen. = Manter as proporções do jogo ou esticar a imagem para preencher o ecrã.
Keeps textures on floors and walls sharp at an angle. = Mantém nítidas as texturas de chãos e paredes vistos de lado.
Shows new pictures at most this often, for even motion. The game runs as before. = Mostra imagens novas no máximo com esta frequência, para um movimento uniforme. O jogo corre como antes.
The emulated console's clock, not your TV. 120 Hz can make games run too fast. = O relógio da consola emulada, não do televisor. 120 Hz pode pôr jogos a correr depressa demais.
Lighter tracking of data the game rewrites all the time. Off only to compare. = Acompanhamento mais leve dos dados que o jogo reescreve constantemente. Desligue só para comparar.
How much memory is sent to video again after the game writes to it. = Quanta memória volta ao vídeo depois de o jogo escrever nela.
Lets the game read back what it drew. Some effects need it; it is slower. = Deixa o jogo ler o que desenhou. Alguns efeitos precisam; é mais lento.
Lets the game read data made by video. Fixes missing objects; it is slower. = Deixa o jogo ler dados feitos pelo vídeo. Corrige objetos em falta; é mais lento.
Prepares new effects in the background: fewer stops, effects may appear late. = Prepara efeitos novos em segundo plano: menos paragens, efeitos podem aparecer mais tarde.
A fix for Team Ninja games where characters disappear. It is slower. = Correção para jogos da Team Ninja em que personagens desaparecem. É mais lento.
Draws from memory the game already gave back, so nothing goes missing. = Desenha a partir de memória que o jogo já libertou, para nada faltar.

# Fix 148h: an install that stopped (the library's message after the dirty-disc error).
Install data incomplete = Dados de instalação incompletos
%s: its install data is incomplete (%s: %s of %s). In the library press Cross on the game, open the %s tab, select each of its install packages and press Triangle (%s). Then start the game to install it again. = %s: os dados de instalação estão incompletos (%s: %s de %s). Na biblioteca, prima Cruz no jogo, abra o separador "%s", selecione cada pacote de instalação e prima Triângulo "%s". Depois inicie o jogo para voltar a instalar.
The video mode the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game; it is not your TV's resolution. It applies when the game starts. At 1080p or 900p some games draw more pixels (sharper, heavier); at 480p some draw fewer and run lighter. 540p is not a mode a real console reports: the game is told 720p. Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = O modo de vídeo que o Xbox 360 emulado indica ao jogo; não é a resolução do televisor. Aplica-se quando o jogo arranca. A 1080p ou 900p alguns jogos desenham mais píxeis (mais nítido, mais pesado); a 480p alguns desenham menos e ficam mais leves. 540p não é um modo de uma consola real: o jogo recebe 720p. Muitos jogos ignoram-no e ficam a 720p.

# Fix 119: the Kinect colour camera from the phone (camera_picture.hpp).
Camera picture for the game = Imagen de la cámara para el juego
With a phone as the Kinect, the phone can also send a small picture from its camera for games that show the camera (replays, photos). It goes only to this console, and the emulator keeps it in memory only, never on a drive; a game may still keep photos of its own in its saves. It also needs Camera picture for the game on the phone page. Off, games see a grey picture. = Con un móvil como Kinect, el móvil también puede enviar una imagen pequeña de su cámara para los juegos que muestran la cámara (repeticiones, fotos). Va solo a esta consola, y el emulador la guarda solo en la memoria, nunca en una unidad; un juego aún puede conservar sus propias fotos en sus partidas guardadas. También hay que activar Imagen de la cámara para el juego en la página del móvil. Desactivada, los juegos ven una imagen gris.
Resolution scale (test) = Escala de renderizado (prueba)
Draws the game at twice its own resolution for a sharper picture. Uses much more video memory and graphics power; some effects (glow, shadows, pictures the game reads back) may look wrong. A test: if the game closes or looks broken, go back to 1x. = Dibuja el juego al doble de su resolución para una imagen más nítida. Usa mucha más memoria de vídeo y potencia gráfica; algunos efectos (brillos, sombras, imágenes que el juego vuelve a leer) pueden verse mal. Es una prueba: si el juego se cierra o se ve mal, vuelve a 1x.
Resolution scale (test) 2x: this game needs more memory than is left. The picture may break; set it to 1x in this game's settings. = Escala de renderizado (prueba) 2x: este juego necesita más memoria de la que queda. La imagen puede fallar; pon 1x en los ajustes de este juego.

# Fix 137: anti-aliasing (FXAA) in the Vulkan swap (launcher option antialiasing).
Anti-aliasing = Suavizado de bordes
Smooths the jagged edges of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. FXAA: light, keeps text sharp. FXAA Extreme: smoother edges, a slightly softer picture. Vulkan only; it costs little. SMAA 1x: cleaner edges than FXAA with less blur, a little heavier. = Suaviza los bordes dentados de la imagen del juego antes de escalarla a la pantalla. FXAA: ligero, mantiene el texto nítido. FXAA Extremo: bordes más suaves, imagen algo más blanda. Solo con Vulkan; cuesta poco. SMAA 1x: bordes más limpios que FXAA y con menos desenfoque, algo más exigente.
# Fix 145: SMAA 1x, the fourth anti-aliasing choice.
SMAA 1x = SMAA 1x
FXAA = FXAA
FXAA Extreme = FXAA Extremo
Anti-aliasing works with the Vulkan renderer only. = El suavizado de bordes solo funciona con el renderizador Vulkan.
# Fix 141: the colour preset and the overscan crop in the Vulkan swap (color_preset, overscan_crop).
Color preset = Ajuste de color
Changes the colors of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. OLED Deep Black: deeper blacks, shadow detail kept. Vibrant: richer colors. Clean Neutral: a slightly brighter, clearer picture. Vulkan only. = Cambia los colores de la imagen del juego antes de escalarla a la pantalla. OLED negro profundo: negros más profundos, sin perder detalle en las sombras. Vívido: colores más ricos. Neutro limpio: imagen algo más clara y nítida. Solo con Vulkan.
OLED Deep Black = OLED negro profundo
Vibrant = Vívido
Clean Neutral = Neutro limpio
Overscan crop = Recorte de overscan
Cuts a thin border off each edge of the game's picture and enlarges the rest, keeping its proportions: for games that show a stripe or garbage at the edges. Vulkan only. = Recorta un borde fino de cada lado de la imagen del juego y amplía el resto, manteniendo sus proporciones: para juegos que muestran una franja o basura en los bordes. Solo con Vulkan.
Color presets and overscan crop work with the Vulkan renderer only. = Los ajustes de color y el recorte de overscan solo funcionan con el renderizador Vulkan.
900p (1600x900) = 900p (1600x900)
The resolution the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game. At 480p or 540p some games draw fewer pixels and run lighter; at 900p or 1080p some draw more (sharper, heavier). Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = La resolución que la Xbox 360 emulada informa al juego. En 480p o 540p algunos juegos dibujan menos puntos y van más ligeros; en 900p o 1080p algunos dibujan más (más nítido, más pesado). Muchos juegos la ignoran y siguen en 720p.
Frame rate limit (screen) = Límite de fotogramas (pantalla)
Shows the game's new pictures at most this many times a second, each for the same time, for an even motion on screen. The game itself runs as before (its speed, its sound and the Emulated video rate do not change), and a game slower than the limit is shown as it is. 40 needs the 120 Hz output (Frame generation output: Up to 120 Hz); at 60 Hz it shows every picture. It does nothing while frame generation is on. Automatic: as before. = Muestra las imágenes nuevas del juego como mucho este número de veces por segundo, cada una durante el mismo tiempo, para un movimiento uniforme en pantalla. El juego sigue funcionando como antes (su velocidad, su sonido y el Ritmo del vídeo emulado no cambian), y un juego más lento que el límite se muestra tal cual. 40 necesita la salida de 120 Hz (Salida de vídeo de la generación: Hasta 120 Hz); a 60 Hz muestra todas las imágenes. No hace nada con la generación de fotogramas activada. Automático: como antes.
No limit = Sin límite

Picture and smoothness = Imagen y fluidez
Emulation (advanced) = Emulación (avanzado)
Emulated video rate (game clock, not your TV) = Ritmo del vídeo emulado (reloj del juego, no de la TV)
The emulated Xbox 360's own clock: how many times a second it tells the game a new frame is due. It is not your TV's refresh rate (that is Frame generation output, under Picture and smoothness). At 120 Hz some games held at 30 frames go to 60, but others run too fast. A test, game by game. = El reloj propio de la Xbox 360 emulada: cuántas veces por segundo avisa al juego de que toca un fotograma nuevo. No es la frecuencia de tu televisor (esa es la Salida de vídeo de la generación, en Imagen y fluidez). A 120 Hz algunos juegos limitados a 30 fotogramas pasan a 60, pero otros van demasiado rápido. Una prueba, juego a juego.
VSync (emulated, game speed) = VSync (emulado, velocidad del juego)
The emulated console's vertical sync, not your TV's. On: games show at most 60 frames a second, so games with a 60 FPS patch do not speed up. Off: no cap, and some games run too fast. = La sincronización vertical de la consola emulada, no la de tu televisor. Activado: los juegos muestran como mucho 60 fotogramas por segundo, así que los juegos con parche de 60 FPS no se aceleran. Desactivado: sin límite, y algunos juegos van demasiado rápido.
How the game's picture looks and how smoothly it is shown: image filter, anti-aliasing, colors, frame rate limit, frame generation and the rate sent to your TV. The game itself runs the same. = Cómo se ve la imagen del juego y con qué fluidez se muestra: filtro de imagen, suavizado de bordes, colores, límite de fotogramas, generación de fotogramas y la frecuencia enviada a tu televisor. El juego en sí funciona igual.
How the emulated Xbox 360 runs each game: its video rate and resolution, VSync, the renderer, memory, readbacks, shaders and tests. They can change a game's speed or correctness; change them game by game. = Cómo ejecuta cada juego la Xbox 360 emulada: ritmo del vídeo y resolución, VSync, renderizador, memoria, lecturas de vuelta, shaders y pruebas. Pueden cambiar la velocidad o la corrección de un juego; cámbialas juego a juego.
Puts every option of the Picture and smoothness, Audio, Controls, System and Emulation (advanced) pages back to its recommended value. Each game's own settings stay as they are. = Devuelve cada opción de las páginas Imagen y fluidez, Audio, Controles, Sistema y Emulación (avanzado) a su valor recomendado. Los ajustes propios de cada juego se quedan como están.

Lets the game read back what it drew. Some effects need it; it is slower. = Deja al juego leer lo que ha dibujado. Algunos efectos lo necesitan; es más lento.

Then start the game to install it again. = %s: los datos de instalación están incompletos (%s: %s de %s). En la biblioteca, pulsa Cruz sobre el juego, abre la pestaña "%s", selecciona cada paquete de instalación y pulsa Triángulo "%s". Después inicia el juego para volver a instalarlo.
The video mode the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game; it is not your TV's resolution. It applies when the game starts. At 1080p or 900p some games draw more pixels (sharper, heavier); at 480p some draw fewer and run lighter. 540p is not a mode a real console reports: the game is told 720p. Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = El modo de vídeo que la Xbox 360 emulada indica al juego; no es la resolución de tu televisor. Se aplica al iniciar el juego. A 1080p o 900p algunos juegos dibujan más píxeles (más nítido, más pesado); a 480p algunos dibujan menos y van más ligeros. 540p no es un modo de una consola real: el juego recibe 720p. Muchos juegos lo ignoran y se quedan en 720p.

# Fix 119: the Kinect colour camera from the phone (camera_picture.hpp).
Camera picture for the game = Imagen de la cámara para el juego
With a phone as the Kinect, the phone can also send a small picture from its camera for games that show the camera (replays, photos). It goes only to this console, and the emulator keeps it in memory only, never on a drive; a game may still keep photos of its own in its saves. It also needs Camera picture for the game on the phone page. Off, games see a grey picture. = Con un celular como Kinect, el celular también puede enviar una imagen pequeña de su cámara para los juegos que muestran la cámara (repeticiones, fotos). Va solo a esta consola, y el emulador la guarda solo en la memoria, nunca en una unidad; un juego aún puede conservar sus propias fotos en sus partidas guardadas. También hay que activar Imagen de la cámara para el juego en la página del celular. Desactivada, los juegos ven una imagen gris.
Resolution scale (test) = Escala de renderizado (prueba)
Draws the game at twice its own resolution for a sharper picture. Uses much more video memory and graphics power; some effects (glow, shadows, pictures the game reads back) may look wrong. A test: if the game closes or looks broken, go back to 1x. = Dibuja el juego al doble de su resolución para una imagen más nítida. Usa mucha más memoria de video y potencia gráfica; algunos efectos (brillos, sombras, imágenes que el juego vuelve a leer) pueden verse mal. Es una prueba: si el juego se cierra o se ve mal, regresa a 1x.
Resolution scale (test) 2x: this game needs more memory than is left. The picture may break; set it to 1x in this game's settings. = Escala de renderizado (prueba) 2x: este juego necesita más memoria de la que queda. La imagen puede fallar; elige 1x en la configuración de este juego.

# Fix 137: anti-aliasing (FXAA) in the Vulkan swap (launcher option antialiasing).
Anti-aliasing = Suavizado de bordes
Smooths the jagged edges of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. FXAA: light, keeps text sharp. FXAA Extreme: smoother edges, a slightly softer picture. Vulkan only; it costs little. SMAA 1x: cleaner edges than FXAA with less blur, a little heavier. = Suaviza los bordes dentados de la imagen del juego antes de escalarla a la pantalla. FXAA: ligero, mantiene el texto nítido. FXAA Extremo: bordes más suaves, imagen un poco más suave. Solo con Vulkan; cuesta poco. SMAA 1x: bordes más limpios que FXAA y con menos desenfoque, un poco más exigente.
# Fix 145: SMAA 1x, the fourth anti-aliasing choice.
SMAA 1x = SMAA 1x
FXAA = FXAA
FXAA Extreme = FXAA Extremo
Anti-aliasing works with the Vulkan renderer only. = El suavizado de bordes solo funciona con el renderizador Vulkan.
# Fix 141: the colour preset and the overscan crop in the Vulkan swap (color_preset, overscan_crop).
Color preset = Ajuste de color
Changes the colors of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. OLED Deep Black: deeper blacks, shadow detail kept. Vibrant: richer colors. Clean Neutral: a slightly brighter, clearer picture. Vulkan only. = Cambia los colores de la imagen del juego antes de escalarla a la pantalla. OLED negro profundo: negros más profundos, sin perder detalle en las sombras. Vívido: colores más ricos. Neutro limpio: imagen un poco más clara y nítida. Solo con Vulkan.
OLED Deep Black = OLED negro profundo
Vibrant = Vívido
Clean Neutral = Neutro limpio
Overscan crop = Recorte de overscan
Cuts a thin border off each edge of the game's picture and enlarges the rest, keeping its proportions: for games that show a stripe or garbage at the edges. Vulkan only. = Recorta un borde delgado de cada lado de la imagen del juego y amplía el resto, manteniendo sus proporciones: para juegos que muestran una franja o basura en los bordes. Solo con Vulkan.
Color presets and overscan crop work with the Vulkan renderer only. = Los ajustes de color y el recorte de overscan solo funcionan con el renderizador Vulkan.
900p (1600x900) = 900p (1600x900)
The resolution the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game. At 480p or 540p some games draw fewer pixels and run lighter; at 900p or 1080p some draw more (sharper, heavier). Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = La resolución que la Xbox 360 emulada informa al juego. En 480p o 540p algunos juegos dibujan menos puntos y van más ligeros; en 900p o 1080p algunos dibujan más (más nítido, más pesado). Muchos juegos la ignoran y siguen en 720p.
Frame rate limit (screen) = Límite de fotogramas (pantalla)
Shows the game's new pictures at most this many times a second, each for the same time, for an even motion on screen. The game itself runs as before (its speed, its sound and the Emulated video rate do not change), and a game slower than the limit is shown as it is. 40 needs the 120 Hz output (Frame generation output: Up to 120 Hz); at 60 Hz it shows every picture. It does nothing while frame generation is on. Automatic: as before. = Muestra las imágenes nuevas del juego como mucho esta cantidad de veces por segundo, cada una durante el mismo tiempo, para un movimiento uniforme en pantalla. El juego sigue funcionando como antes (su velocidad, su sonido y el Ritmo del video emulado no cambian), y un juego más lento que el límite se muestra tal cual. 40 necesita la salida de 120 Hz (Salida de video de la generación de fotogramas: Hasta 120 Hz); a 60 Hz muestra todas las imágenes. No hace nada con la generación de fotogramas activada. Automático: como antes.
No limit = Sin límite

Keys refused (no fingerprint matches): %u = Claves rechazadas (ninguna huella coincide): %u
Picture and smoothness = Imagen y fluidez
Emulation (advanced) = Emulación (avanzado)
Emulated video rate (game clock, not your TV) = Ritmo del video emulado (reloj del juego, no de la TV)
The emulated Xbox 360's own clock: how many times a second it tells the game a new frame is due. It is not your TV's refresh rate (that is Frame generation output, under Picture and smoothness). At 120 Hz some games held at 30 frames go to 60, but others run too fast. A test, game by game. = El reloj propio de la Xbox 360 emulada: cuántas veces por segundo le avisa al juego que toca un fotograma nuevo. No es la frecuencia de tu TV (esa es la Salida de video de la generación de fotogramas, en Imagen y fluidez). A 120 Hz algunos juegos limitados a 30 fotogramas pasan a 60, pero otros van demasiado rápido. Una prueba, juego por juego.
VSync (emulated, game speed) = VSync (emulado, velocidad del juego)
The emulated console's vertical sync, not your TV's. On: games show at most 60 frames a second, so games with a 60 FPS patch do not speed up. Off: no cap, and some games run too fast. = La sincronización vertical de la consola emulada, no la de tu TV. Activado: los juegos muestran como máximo 60 fotogramas por segundo, así que los juegos con parche de 60 FPS no se aceleran. Desactivado: sin límite, y algunos juegos van demasiado rápido.
How the game's picture looks and how smoothly it is shown: image filter, anti-aliasing, colors, frame rate limit, frame generation and the rate sent to your TV. The game itself runs the same. = Cómo se ve la imagen del juego y con qué fluidez se muestra: filtro de imagen, suavizado de bordes, colores, límite de fotogramas, generación de fotogramas y la frecuencia que se envía a tu TV. El juego en sí funciona igual.
How the emulated Xbox 360 runs each game: its video rate and resolution, VSync, the renderer, memory, readbacks, shaders and tests. They can change a game's speed or correctness; change them game by game. = Cómo ejecuta cada juego la Xbox 360 emulada: ritmo del video y resolución, VSync, renderizador, memoria, lecturas de vuelta, shaders y pruebas. Pueden cambiar la velocidad o la corrección de un juego; cámbialas juego por juego.
Puts every option of the Picture and smoothness, Audio, Controls, System and Emulation (advanced) pages back to its recommended value. Each game's own settings stay as they are. = Devuelve cada opción de las páginas Imagen y fluidez, Audio, Controles, Sistema y Emulación (avanzado) a su valor recomendado. La configuración propia de cada juego queda como está.

The emulated console's clock, not your TV. 120 Hz can make games run too fast. = El reloj de la consola emulada, no de tu TV. 120 Hz puede hacer que los juegos vayan demasiado rápido.
Lighter tracking of data the game rewrites all the time. Off only to compare. = Seguimiento más liviano de los datos que el juego reescribe todo el tiempo. Desactívalo solo para comparar.

Lets the game read back what it drew. Some effects need it; it is slower. = Permite que el juego lea lo que dibujó. Algunos efectos lo necesitan; es más lento.

# Fix 148h: an install that stopped (the library's message after the dirty-disc error).
Install data incomplete = Datos de instalación incompletos
%s: its install data is incomplete (%s: %s of %s). In the library press Cross on the game, open the %s tab, select each of its install packages and press Triangle (%s). Then start the game to install it again. = %s: los datos de instalación están incompletos (%s: %s de %s). En la biblioteca, presiona Cruz sobre el juego, abre la pestaña "%s", selecciona cada paquete de instalación y presiona Triángulo "%s". Después inicia el juego para volver a instalarlo.
The video mode the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game; it is not your TV's resolution. It applies when the game starts. At 1080p or 900p some games draw more pixels (sharper, heavier); at 480p some draw fewer and run lighter. 540p is not a mode a real console reports: the game is told 720p. Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = El modo de video que la Xbox 360 emulada le informa al juego; no es la resolución de tu TV. Se aplica al iniciar el juego. A 1080p o 900p algunos juegos dibujan más píxeles (más nítido, más pesado); a 480p algunos dibujan menos y funcionan más livianos. 540p no es un modo de una consola real: el juego recibe 720p. Muchos juegos lo ignoran y se quedan en 720p.

# Fix 119: the Kinect colour camera from the phone (camera_picture.hpp).
Camera picture for the game = Kamerabild für das Spiel
With a phone as the Kinect, the phone can also send a small picture from its camera for games that show the camera (replays, photos). It goes only to this console, and the emulator keeps it in memory only, never on a drive; a game may still keep photos of its own in its saves. It also needs Camera picture for the game on the phone page. Off, games see a grey picture. = Mit einem Handy als Kinect kann das Handy auch ein kleines Bild seiner Kamera senden, für Spiele, die die Kamera zeigen (Wiederholungen, Fotos). Es geht nur an diese Konsole, und der Emulator hält es nur im Arbeitsspeicher, nie auf einem Laufwerk; ein Spiel kann trotzdem eigene Fotos in seinen Spielständen behalten. Dazu muss auch Kamerabild für das Spiel auf der Handy-Seite eingeschaltet sein. Aus sehen Spiele ein graues Bild.
Resolution scale (test) = Renderskalierung (Test)
Draws the game at twice its own resolution for a sharper picture. Uses much more video memory and graphics power; some effects (glow, shadows, pictures the game reads back) may look wrong. A test: if the game closes or looks broken, go back to 1x. = Zeichnet das Spiel mit der doppelten eigenen Auflösung für ein schärferes Bild. Braucht viel mehr Videospeicher und Grafikleistung; manche Effekte (Leuchten, Schatten, Bilder, die das Spiel zurückliest) können falsch aussehen. Ein Test: Wenn sich das Spiel schließt oder fehlerhaft aussieht, zurück auf 1x stellen.
Resolution scale (test) 2x: this game needs more memory than is left. The picture may break; set it to 1x in this game's settings. = Renderskalierung (Test) 2x: Dieses Spiel braucht mehr Speicher, als noch frei ist. Das Bild kann fehlerhaft werden; stelle in den Einstellungen dieses Spiels 1x ein.

# Fix 137: anti-aliasing (FXAA) in the Vulkan swap (launcher option antialiasing).
Anti-aliasing = Kantenglättung
Smooths the jagged edges of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. FXAA: light, keeps text sharp. FXAA Extreme: smoother edges, a slightly softer picture. Vulkan only; it costs little. SMAA 1x: cleaner edges than FXAA with less blur, a little heavier. = Glättet die gezackten Kanten des Spielbilds, bevor es auf den Bildschirm skaliert wird. FXAA: leicht, Text bleibt scharf. FXAA Extrem: glattere Kanten, etwas weicheres Bild. Nur mit Vulkan; kostet wenig. SMAA 1x: sauberere Kanten als FXAA mit weniger Unschärfe, etwas aufwendiger.
# Fix 145: SMAA 1x, the fourth anti-aliasing choice.
SMAA 1x = SMAA 1x
FXAA = FXAA
FXAA Extreme = FXAA Extrem
Anti-aliasing works with the Vulkan renderer only. = Kantenglättung funktioniert nur mit dem Vulkan-Renderer.
# Fix 141: the colour preset and the overscan crop in the Vulkan swap (color_preset, overscan_crop).
Color preset = Farbvoreinstellung
Changes the colors of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. OLED Deep Black: deeper blacks, shadow detail kept. Vibrant: richer colors. Clean Neutral: a slightly brighter, clearer picture. Vulkan only. = Ändert die Farben des Spielbilds, bevor es auf den Bildschirm skaliert wird. OLED Tiefschwarz: tieferes Schwarz, Details in den Schatten bleiben. Lebendig: kräftigere Farben. Klar neutral: ein etwas helleres, klareres Bild. Nur mit Vulkan.
OLED Deep Black = OLED Tiefschwarz
Vibrant = Lebendig
Clean Neutral = Klar neutral
Overscan crop = Overscan-Beschnitt
Cuts a thin border off each edge of the game's picture and enlarges the rest, keeping its proportions: for games that show a stripe or garbage at the edges. Vulkan only. = Schneidet an jedem Rand des Spielbilds einen schmalen Streifen ab und vergrößert den Rest, ohne die Proportionen zu ändern: für Spiele mit einem Streifen oder Bildmüll an den Rändern. Nur mit Vulkan.
Color presets and overscan crop work with the Vulkan renderer only. = Farbvoreinstellungen und Overscan-Beschnitt funktionieren nur mit dem Vulkan-Renderer.
900p (1600x900) = 900p (1600x900)
The resolution the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game. At 480p or 540p some games draw fewer pixels and run lighter; at 900p or 1080p some draw more (sharper, heavier). Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = Die Auflösung, die die emulierte Xbox 360 dem Spiel meldet. Bei 480p oder 540p zeichnen manche Spiele weniger Pixel und laufen leichter; bei 900p oder 1080p zeichnen manche mehr (schärfer, schwerer). Viele Spiele ignorieren sie und bleiben bei 720p.
Frame rate limit (screen) = Bildratenlimit (Bildschirm)
Shows the game's new pictures at most this many times a second, each for the same time, for an even motion on screen. The game itself runs as before (its speed, its sound and the Emulated video rate do not change), and a game slower than the limit is shown as it is. 40 needs the 120 Hz output (Frame generation output: Up to 120 Hz); at 60 Hz it shows every picture. It does nothing while frame generation is on. Automatic: as before. = Zeigt neue Bilder des Spiels höchstens so oft pro Sekunde, jedes gleich lange, für eine gleichmäßige Bewegung auf dem Bildschirm. Das Spiel selbst läuft wie bisher (Tempo, Ton und die Emulierte Bildrate ändern sich nicht), und ein Spiel, das langsamer als das Limit ist, wird so gezeigt, wie es ist. 40 braucht die 120-Hz-Ausgabe (Ausgabe der Bildgenerierung: Bis zu 120 Hz); bei 60 Hz zeigt es jedes Bild. Bei eingeschalteter Bildgenerierung wirkt es nicht. Automatisch: wie bisher.
No limit = Kein Limit

Picture and smoothness = Bild und Flüssigkeit
Emulation (advanced) = Emulation (erweitert)
Emulated video rate (game clock, not your TV) = Emulierte Bildrate (Spieltakt, nicht der Fernseher)
The emulated Xbox 360's own clock: how many times a second it tells the game a new frame is due. It is not your TV's refresh rate (that is Frame generation output, under Picture and smoothness). At 120 Hz some games held at 30 frames go to 60, but others run too fast. A test, game by game. = Der eigene Takt der emulierten Xbox 360: wie oft pro Sekunde sie dem Spiel meldet, dass ein neues Bild fällig ist. Das ist nicht die Bildwiederholrate des Fernsehers (das ist die Ausgabe der Bildgenerierung, unter Bild und Flüssigkeit). Bei 120 Hz gehen manche auf 30 Bilder begrenzte Spiele auf 60, andere laufen aber zu schnell. Ein Test, Spiel für Spiel.
VSync (emulated, game speed) = VSync (emuliert, Spieltempo)
The emulated console's vertical sync, not your TV's. On: games show at most 60 frames a second, so games with a 60 FPS patch do not speed up. Off: no cap, and some games run too fast. = Die vertikale Synchronisation der emulierten Konsole, nicht die des Fernsehers. An: Spiele zeigen höchstens 60 Bilder pro Sekunde, sodass Spiele mit 60-FPS-Patch nicht schneller werden. Aus: keine Grenze, und manche Spiele laufen zu schnell.
How the game's picture looks and how smoothly it is shown: image filter, anti-aliasing, colors, frame rate limit, frame generation and the rate sent to your TV. The game itself runs the same. = Wie das Bild des Spiels aussieht und wie flüssig es gezeigt wird: Bildfilter, Kantenglättung, Farben, Bildratenlimit, Bildgenerierung und die an den Fernseher gesendete Rate. Das Spiel selbst läuft gleich.
How the emulated Xbox 360 runs each game: its video rate and resolution, VSync, the renderer, memory, readbacks, shaders and tests. They can change a game's speed or correctness; change them game by game. = Wie die emulierte Xbox 360 jedes Spiel ausführt: Bildrate und Auflösung, VSync, Renderer, Speicher, Rücklesen, Shader und Tests. Sie können Tempo oder Korrektheit eines Spiels ändern; am besten Spiel für Spiel ändern.
Puts every option of the Picture and smoothness, Audio, Controls, System and Emulation (advanced) pages back to its recommended value. Each game's own settings stay as they are. = Setzt jede Option der Seiten Bild und Flüssigkeit, Audio, Steuerung, System und Emulation (erweitert) auf ihren empfohlenen Wert zurück. Die eigenen Einstellungen jedes Spiels bleiben, wie sie sind.
Anti-aliasing and sharpness = Kantenglättung und Schärfe

Each identified game's own settings, in the same pages as these. What a game does not change follows these general settings. = Die eigenen Einstellungen jedes erkannten Spiels, auf denselben Seiten wie diese. Was ein Spiel nicht ändert, folgt diesen allgemeinen Einstellungen.
Covers, game folders, the game list and saved data. = Cover, Spielordner, die Spieleliste und gespeicherte Daten.
Each game can have its own picture, audio, controls, system and emulation settings. What it does not change follows the general settings. Was es nicht ändert, folgt den allgemeinen Einstellungen. In der Bibliothek öffnet Dreieck auf einem Spiel dieselben Einstellungen.
Each game can have its own value: Settings > Game settings. = Jedes Spiel kann einen eigenen Wert haben: Einstellungen > Spieleinstellungen.
These options change how the game runs. = Diese Optionen ändern, wie das Spiel läuft.
Changed for this game. Too fast or wrong? Set it back. = Für dieses Spiel geändert. Zu schnell oder fehlerhaft? Zurückstellen.
Changes how the game runs: it can run faster, slower or wrong. = Ändert, wie das Spiel läuft: es kann schneller, langsamer oder fehlerhaft laufen.
How the picture is scaled to your screen. CAS sharpens; FSR upscales with cleaner edges. = Wie das Bild an den Bildschirm angepasst wird. CAS schärft; FSR skaliert mit saubereren Kanten.
Smooths jagged edges. FXAA is light; SMAA 1x gives the cleanest edges. = Glättet gezackte Kanten. FXAA ist leicht; SMAA 1x gibt die saubersten Kanten.
Changes the picture's colors: deeper blacks, richer or brighter colors. = Ändert die Farben des Bildes: tieferes Schwarz, kräftigere oder hellere Farben.
Cuts a thin border off the edges, for games with a stripe at the edge. = Schneidet einen schmalen Rand ab, für Spiele mit einem Streifen am Rand.
How strongly CAS and FSR sharpen the picture. = Wie stark CAS und FSR das Bild schärfen.
Hides banding in skies and shadows. = Verbirgt Streifen in Himmel und Schatten.
Keep the game's proportions, or stretch the picture to fill the screen. = Die Proportionen des Spiels behalten oder das Bild auf den ganzen Bildschirm strecken.
Keeps textures on floors and walls sharp at an angle. = Hält Texturen von Böden und Wänden auch schräg gesehen scharf.
Shows new pictures at most this often, for even motion. The game runs as before. = Zeigt neue Bilder höchstens so oft, für gleichmäßige Bewegung. Das Spiel läuft wie bisher.
The emulated console's clock, not your TV. 120 Hz can make games run too fast. = Der Takt der emulierten Konsole, nicht des Fernsehers. 120 Hz kann Spiele zu schnell laufen lassen.
Lighter tracking of data the game rewrites all the time. Off only to compare. = Leichtere Verfolgung von Daten, die das Spiel ständig neu schreibt. Nur zum Vergleich ausschalten.
How much memory is sent to video again after the game writes to it. = Wie viel Speicher nach einem Schreiben des Spiels erneut ans Video geht.
Lets the game read back what it drew. Some effects need it; it is slower. = Lässt das Spiel zurücklesen, was es gezeichnet hat. Manche Effekte brauchen das; langsamer.
Lets the game read data made by video. Fixes missing objects; it is slower. = Lässt das Spiel Daten lesen, die das Video erzeugt. Behebt fehlende Objekte; langsamer.
Prepares new effects in the background: fewer stops, effects may appear late. = Bereitet neue Effekte im Hintergrund vor: weniger Stocken, Effekte erscheinen eventuell später.
A fix for Team Ninja games where characters disappear. It is slower. = Eine Korrektur für Team-Ninja-Spiele, in denen Figuren verschwinden. Langsamer.
Draws from memory the game already gave back, so nothing goes missing. = Zeichnet aus Speicher, den das Spiel schon freigegeben hat, damit nichts fehlt.
Turns the game's sound off or on. = Schaltet den Spielton aus oder ein.
The game's sound volume. The TV's volume still applies. = Die Lautstärke des Spieltons. Die Lautstärke des Fernsehers gilt weiterhin.

# Fix 148h: an install that stopped (the library's message after the dirty-disc error).
Install data incomplete = Installationsdaten unvollständig
%s: its install data is incomplete (%s: %s of %s). In the library press Cross on the game, open the %s tab, select each of its install packages and press Triangle (%s). Then start the game to install it again. = %s: Die Installationsdaten sind unvollständig (%s: %s von %s). Drücke in der Bibliothek Kreuz auf dem Spiel, öffne den Tab "%s", wähle jedes Installationspaket aus und drücke Dreieck "%s". Starte danach das Spiel, um es neu zu installieren.
The video mode the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game; it is not your TV's resolution. It applies when the game starts. At 1080p or 900p some games draw more pixels (sharper, heavier); at 480p some draw fewer and run lighter. 540p is not a mode a real console reports: the game is told 720p. Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = Der Videomodus, den die emulierte Xbox 360 dem Spiel meldet; das ist nicht die Auflösung des Fernsehers. Er gilt ab dem Spielstart. Bei 1080p oder 900p zeichnen manche Spiele mehr Pixel (schärfer, schwerer); bei 480p zeichnen manche weniger und laufen leichter. 540p ist kein Modus einer echten Konsole: Das Spiel bekommt 720p. Viele Spiele ignorieren ihn und bleiben bei 720p.

Logs for a bug report = Journaux pour un rapport de bug
For the next game you start, the emulator writes down more of what happens. Play until the problem shows, then close the game. A screen then lists the files to attach to your bug report. It turns itself off after that game. = Pour le prochain jeu que vous lancez, l'émulateur note plus de détails sur ce qui se passe. Jouez jusqu'à ce que le problème apparaisse, puis fermez le jeu. Un écran liste ensuite les fichiers à joindre à votre rapport de bug. L'option se désactive d'elle-même après ce jeu.

# Fix 119: the Kinect colour camera from the phone (camera_picture.hpp).
Camera picture for the game = Image de la caméra pour le jeu
With a phone as the Kinect, the phone can also send a small picture from its camera for games that show the camera (replays, photos). It goes only to this console, and the emulator keeps it in memory only, never on a drive; a game may still keep photos of its own in its saves. It also needs Camera picture for the game on the phone page. Off, games see a grey picture. = Avec un téléphone comme Kinect, le téléphone peut aussi envoyer une petite image de sa caméra pour les jeux qui montrent la caméra (replays, photos). Elle ne va qu'à cette console, et l'émulateur la garde seulement en mémoire, jamais sur un disque ; un jeu peut quand même garder ses propres photos dans ses sauvegardes. Il faut aussi activer Image de la caméra pour le jeu sur la page du téléphone. Désactivée, les jeux voient une image grise.
Resolution scale (test) = Échelle de rendu (test)
Draws the game at twice its own resolution for a sharper picture. Uses much more video memory and graphics power; some effects (glow, shadows, pictures the game reads back) may look wrong. A test: if the game closes or looks broken, go back to 1x. = Dessine le jeu au double de sa résolution pour une image plus nette. Utilise beaucoup plus de mémoire vidéo et de puissance graphique ; certains effets (lueurs, ombres, images que le jeu relit) peuvent être incorrects. C'est un test : si le jeu se ferme ou s'affiche mal, revenez à 1x.
Resolution scale (test) 2x: this game needs more memory than is left. The picture may break; set it to 1x in this game's settings. = Échelle de rendu (test) 2x : ce jeu a besoin de plus de mémoire qu'il n'en reste. L'image peut se dégrader ; choisissez 1x dans les réglages de ce jeu.

# Fix 137: anti-aliasing (FXAA) in the Vulkan swap (launcher option antialiasing).
Anti-aliasing = Anticrénelage
Smooths the jagged edges of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. FXAA: light, keeps text sharp. FXAA Extreme: smoother edges, a slightly softer picture. Vulkan only; it costs little. SMAA 1x: cleaner edges than FXAA with less blur, a little heavier. = Adoucit les bords crénelés de l'image du jeu avant sa mise à l'échelle à l'écran. FXAA : léger, garde le texte net. FXAA Extrême : bords plus lisses, image un peu plus douce. Vulkan uniquement ; coûte peu. SMAA 1x : bords plus nets qu'avec FXAA et moins de flou, un peu plus exigeant.
# Fix 145: SMAA 1x, the fourth anti-aliasing choice.
SMAA 1x = SMAA 1x
FXAA = FXAA
FXAA Extreme = FXAA Extrême
Anti-aliasing works with the Vulkan renderer only. = L'anticrénelage ne fonctionne qu'avec le moteur de rendu Vulkan.
# Fix 141: the colour preset and the overscan crop in the Vulkan swap (color_preset, overscan_crop).
Color preset = Préréglage de couleurs
Changes the colors of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. OLED Deep Black: deeper blacks, shadow detail kept. Vibrant: richer colors. Clean Neutral: a slightly brighter, clearer picture. Vulkan only. = Modifie les couleurs de l'image du jeu avant sa mise à l'échelle à l'écran. OLED noir profond : noirs plus profonds, détails des ombres conservés. Éclatant : couleurs plus riches. Neutre net : image un peu plus claire et plus nette. Vulkan uniquement.
OLED Deep Black = OLED noir profond
Vibrant = Éclatant
Clean Neutral = Neutre net
Overscan crop = Recadrage overscan
Cuts a thin border off each edge of the game's picture and enlarges the rest, keeping its proportions: for games that show a stripe or garbage at the edges. Vulkan only. = Retire une fine bordure de chaque bord de l'image du jeu et agrandit le reste en gardant ses proportions : pour les jeux qui affichent une bande ou des artefacts sur les bords. Vulkan uniquement.
Color presets and overscan crop work with the Vulkan renderer only. = Les préréglages de couleurs et le recadrage overscan ne fonctionnent qu'avec le moteur de rendu Vulkan.
900p (1600x900) = 900p (1600x900)
The resolution the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game. At 480p or 540p some games draw fewer pixels and run lighter; at 900p or 1080p some draw more (sharper, heavier). Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = La résolution que la Xbox 360 émulée annonce au jeu. En 480p ou 540p, certains jeux dessinent moins de pixels et sont plus légers ; en 900p ou 1080p, certains en dessinent plus (plus net, plus lourd). Beaucoup de jeux l'ignorent et restent en 720p.
Frame rate limit (screen) = Limite d'images (écran)
Shows the game's new pictures at most this many times a second, each for the same time, for an even motion on screen. The game itself runs as before (its speed, its sound and the Emulated video rate do not change), and a game slower than the limit is shown as it is. 40 needs the 120 Hz output (Frame generation output: Up to 120 Hz); at 60 Hz it shows every picture. It does nothing while frame generation is on. Automatic: as before. = Affiche les nouvelles images du jeu au plus ce nombre de fois par seconde, chacune pendant la même durée, pour un mouvement régulier à l'écran. Le jeu lui-même tourne comme avant (sa vitesse, son son et la Fréquence vidéo émulée ne changent pas), et un jeu plus lent que la limite est affiché tel quel. 40 nécessite la sortie 120 Hz (Sortie de la génération d'images : Jusqu'à 120 Hz) ; à 60 Hz, toutes les images sont affichées. Sans effet quand la génération d'images est activée. Automatique : comme avant.
No limit = Sans limite
# Fix 147c: the Harmonix music keys (overlay and the /keys page).
Music keys needed = Clés de musique nécessaires
Scan to add the Harmonix music keys (HvKeys). Restart the game after. = Scannez pour ajouter les clés de musique Harmonix (HvKeys). Redémarrez ensuite le jeu.
Where to get them: = Où les trouver :
Harmonix music keys = Clés de musique Harmonix
This game's music is encrypted with Harmonix keys, which are not included. Paste the HvKeys array from keys.h (or one key per line) and save. = La musique de ce jeu est chiffrée avec des clés Harmonix, qui ne sont pas incluses. Collez le tableau HvKeys de keys.h (ou une clé par ligne) et enregistrez.
Save = Enregistrer
Keys saved: %u of %u. Restart the game to use them. = Clés enregistrées : %u sur %u. Redémarrez le jeu pour les utiliser.
Keys refused (no fingerprint matches): %u = Clés refusées (aucune empreinte ne correspond) : %u
Picture and smoothness = Image et fluidité
Emulation (advanced) = Émulation (avancé)
Emulated video rate (game clock, not your TV) = Fréquence vidéo émulée (horloge du jeu, pas votre TV)
The emulated Xbox 360's own clock: how many times a second it tells the game a new frame is due. It is not your TV's refresh rate (that is Frame generation output, under Picture and smoothness). At 120 Hz some games held at 30 frames go to 60, but others run too fast. A test, game by game. = L'horloge propre de la Xbox 360 émulée : combien de fois par seconde elle signale au jeu qu'une nouvelle image est due. Ce n'est pas la fréquence de votre téléviseur (c'est la Sortie de la génération d'images, dans Image et fluidité). À 120 Hz, certains jeux bloqués à 30 images passent à 60, mais d'autres tournent trop vite. Un test, jeu par jeu.
VSync (emulated, game speed) = VSync (émulée, vitesse du jeu)
The emulated console's vertical sync, not your TV's. On: games show at most 60 frames a second, so games with a 60 FPS patch do not speed up. Off: no cap, and some games run too fast. = La synchronisation verticale de la console émulée, pas celle de votre téléviseur. Activée : les jeux affichent au plus 60 images par seconde, donc les jeux avec un patch 60 FPS n'accélèrent pas. Désactivée : sans limite, et certains jeux tournent trop vite.
How the game's picture looks and how smoothly it is shown: image filter, anti-aliasing, colors, frame rate limit, frame generation and the rate sent to your TV. The game itself runs the same. = L'aspect de l'image du jeu et la fluidité de son affichage : filtre d'image, anticrénelage, couleurs, limite d'images, génération d'images et la fréquence envoyée à votre téléviseur. Le jeu lui-même tourne de la même façon.
How the emulated Xbox 360 runs each game: its video rate and resolution, VSync, the renderer, memory, readbacks, shaders and tests. They can change a game's speed or correctness; change them game by game. = La façon dont la Xbox 360 émulée exécute chaque jeu : fréquence vidéo et résolution, VSync, moteur de rendu, mémoire, relectures, shaders et tests. Elles peuvent changer la vitesse ou l'exactitude d'un jeu ; changez-les jeu par jeu.
Puts every option of the Picture and smoothness, Audio, Controls, System and Emulation (advanced) pages back to its recommended value. Each game's own settings stay as they are. = Remet chaque option des pages Image et fluidité, Audio, Commandes, Système et Émulation (avancé) à sa valeur recommandée. Les réglages propres à chaque jeu restent tels quels.
Anti-aliasing and sharpness = Anticrénelage et netteté

Each identified game's own settings, in the same pages as these. What a game does not change follows these general settings. = Les réglages propres à chaque jeu identifié, dans les mêmes pages que ceux-ci. Ce qu'un jeu ne change pas suit ces réglages généraux.
Covers, game folders, the game list and saved data. = Jaquettes, dossiers de jeux, liste des jeux et données sauvegardées.
Each game can have its own picture, audio, controls, system and emulation settings. What it does not change follows the general settings. In the library, Triangle on a game opens the same settings. = Chaque jeu peut avoir ses propres réglages d'image, d'audio, de commandes, de système et d'émulation. Ce qu'il ne change pas suit les réglages généraux. Dans la bibliothèque, Triangle sur un jeu ouvre ces mêmes réglages.
Each game can have its own value: Settings > Game settings. = Chaque jeu peut avoir sa propre valeur : Paramètres > Paramètres du jeu.
These options change how the game runs. = Ces options changent la façon dont le jeu tourne.
Changed for this game. Too fast or wrong? Set it back. = Modifié pour ce jeu. Trop rapide ou faux ? Remettez la valeur.
Changes how the game runs: it can run faster, slower or wrong. = Change la façon dont le jeu tourne : il peut aller plus vite, plus lentement ou mal.
How the picture is scaled to your screen. CAS sharpens; FSR upscales with cleaner edges. = Comment l'image est adaptée à l'écran. CAS l'affine ; FSR l'agrandit avec des bords plus nets.
Smooths jagged edges. FXAA is light; SMAA 1x gives the cleanest edges. = Adoucit les bords crénelés. FXAA est léger ; SMAA 1x donne les bords les plus nets.
Changes the picture's colors: deeper blacks, richer or brighter colors. = Change les couleurs de l'image : noirs plus profonds, couleurs plus vives ou plus claires.
Cuts a thin border off the edges, for games with a stripe at the edge. = Coupe une fine bordure sur les côtés, pour les jeux avec une bande au bord.
How strongly CAS and FSR sharpen the picture. = La force avec laquelle CAS et FSR affinent l'image.
Hides banding in skies and shadows. = Masque les bandes dans les ciels et les ombres.
Keep the game's proportions, or stretch the picture to fill the screen. = Garder les proportions du jeu ou étirer l'image sur tout l'écran.
Keeps textures on floors and walls sharp at an angle. = Garde nettes les textures des sols et des murs vus en biais.
Shows new pictures at most this often, for even motion. The game runs as before. = Affiche de nouvelles images au plus aussi souvent, pour un mouvement régulier. Le jeu tourne comme avant.
The emulated console's clock, not your TV. 120 Hz can make games run too fast. = L'horloge de la console émulée, pas de votre téléviseur. 120 Hz peut faire tourner les jeux trop vite.
Lighter tracking of data the game rewrites all the time. Off only to compare. = Suivi plus léger des données que le jeu réécrit sans cesse. À désactiver seulement pour comparer.
How much memory is sent to video again after the game writes to it. = Quelle quantité de mémoire est renvoyée à la vidéo après une écriture du jeu.
Lets the game read back what it drew. Some effects need it; it is slower. = Permet au jeu de relire ce qu'il a dessiné. Certains effets en ont besoin ; plus lent.
Lets the game read data made by video. Fixes missing objects; it is slower. = Permet au jeu de lire des données faites par la vidéo. Corrige des objets manquants ; plus lent.
Prepares new effects in the background: fewer stops, effects may appear late. = Prépare les nouveaux effets en arrière-plan : moins d'arrêts, les effets peuvent arriver plus tard.
A fix for Team Ninja games where characters disappear. It is slower. = Une correction pour les jeux Team Ninja où des personnages disparaissent. Plus lent.
Draws from memory the game already gave back, so nothing goes missing. = Dessine depuis la mémoire que le jeu a déjà rendue, pour que rien ne manque.
Turns the game's sound off or on. = Coupe ou remet le son du jeu.
The game's sound volume. The TV's volume still applies. = Le volume du son du jeu. Le volume du téléviseur s'applique toujours.
The emulator's menu sounds. = Les sons des menus de l'émulateur.
The volume of the menu sounds. = Le volume des sons des menus.
What a touchpad click does: open this guide, or press Xbox Back. = Ce que fait un clic sur le pavé tactile : ouvrir ce guide ou appuyer sur Xbox Back.
Sends the game's vibration to the controller. = Envoie les vibrations du jeu à la manette.
Ignores small movements of the left stick. = Ignore les petits mouvements du joystick gauche.
Ignores small movements of the right stick. = Ignore les petits mouvements du joystick droit.
A phone as a motion camera (research). It shows diagnostics. = Un téléphone comme caméra de mouvement (recherche). Affiche des diagnostics.
Where the motion panel is drawn over the game. = Où le panneau de mouvement est dessiné sur le jeu.

# Fix 148f: a package shorter than its header says (the DLCs tab).
incomplete: %s of %s = incomplet : %s sur %s
# Fix 148h: an install that stopped (the library's message after the dirty-disc error).
Install data incomplete = Données d'installation incomplètes
%s: its install data is incomplete (%s: %s of %s). In the library press Cross on the game, open the %s tab, select each of its install packages and press Triangle (%s). Then start the game to install it again. = %s : les données d'installation sont incomplètes (%s : %s sur %s). Dans la bibliothèque, appuyez sur Croix sur le jeu, ouvrez l'onglet « %s », sélectionnez chacun de ses paquets d'installation et appuyez sur Triangle « %s ». Lancez ensuite le jeu pour le réinstaller.
The video mode the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game; it is not your TV's resolution. It applies when the game starts. At 1080p or 900p some games draw more pixels (sharper, heavier); at 480p some draw fewer and run lighter. 540p is not a mode a real console reports: the game is told 720p. Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = Le mode vidéo que la Xbox 360 émulée indique au jeu ; ce n'est pas la résolution de votre téléviseur. Il s'applique au lancement du jeu. En 1080p ou 900p, certains jeux dessinent plus de pixels (plus net, plus lourd) ; en 480p, certains en dessinent moins et sont plus légers. 540p n'est pas un mode d'une vraie console : le jeu reçoit 720p. Beaucoup de jeux l'ignorent et restent en 720p.

Logs for a bug report = Log per una segnalazione di bug
For the next game you start, the emulator writes down more of what happens. Play until the problem shows, then close the game. A screen then lists the files to attach to your bug report. It turns itself off after that game. = Nel prossimo gioco che avvii, l'emulatore annota più dettagli su ciò che succede. Gioca finché il problema non si presenta, poi chiudi il gioco. Una schermata elenca quindi i file da allegare alla tua segnalazione di bug. L'opzione si disattiva da sola dopo quel gioco.

# Fix 119: the Kinect colour camera from the phone (camera_picture.hpp).
Camera picture for the game = Immagine della fotocamera per il gioco
With a phone as the Kinect, the phone can also send a small picture from its camera for games that show the camera (replays, photos). It goes only to this console, and the emulator keeps it in memory only, never on a drive; a game may still keep photos of its own in its saves. It also needs Camera picture for the game on the phone page. Off, games see a grey picture. = Con un telefono come Kinect, il telefono può anche inviare una piccola immagine della sua fotocamera per i giochi che mostrano la telecamera (replay, foto). Va solo a questa console, e l'emulatore la tiene solo in memoria, mai su un'unità; un gioco può comunque conservare foto proprie nei suoi salvataggi. Serve anche attivare Immagine della fotocamera per il gioco nella pagina del telefono. Disattivata, i giochi vedono un'immagine grigia.
Resolution scale (test) = Scala di rendering (test)
Draws the game at twice its own resolution for a sharper picture. Uses much more video memory and graphics power; some effects (glow, shadows, pictures the game reads back) may look wrong. A test: if the game closes or looks broken, go back to 1x. = Disegna il gioco al doppio della sua risoluzione per un'immagine più nitida. Usa molta più memoria video e potenza grafica; alcuni effetti (bagliori, ombre, immagini che il gioco rilegge) possono apparire errati. È un test: se il gioco si chiude o appare difettoso, torna a 1x.
Resolution scale (test) 2x: this game needs more memory than is left. The picture may break; set it to 1x in this game's settings. = Scala di rendering (test) 2x: questo gioco richiede più memoria di quella rimasta. L'immagine potrebbe guastarsi; imposta 1x nelle impostazioni di questo gioco.
