Picture and smoothness = Görüntü ve akıcılık
Emulation (advanced) = Emülasyon (gelişmiş)
Emulated video rate (game clock, not your TV) = Emüle edilen görüntü hızı (oyun saati, TV değil)
The emulated Xbox 360's own clock: how many times a second it tells the game a new frame is due. It is not your TV's refresh rate (that is Frame generation output, under Picture and smoothness). At 120 Hz some games held at 30 frames go to 60, but others run too fast. A test, game by game. = Emüle edilen Xbox 360'ın kendi saati: oyuna saniyede kaç kez yeni bir karenin zamanının geldiğini bildirdiği. TV'nin yenileme hızı değildir (o, Görüntü ve akıcılık altındaki Kare üretimi çıkışıdır). 120 Hz'de 30 kareye sabitlenmiş bazı oyunlar 60'a çıkar, ancak diğerleri fazla hızlı çalışır. Oyun oyun bir denemedir.
VSync (emulated, game speed) = VSync (emüle edilen, oyun hızı)
The emulated console's vertical sync, not your TV's. On: games show at most 60 frames a second, so games with a 60 FPS patch do not speed up. Off: no cap, and some games run too fast. = TV'nin değil, emüle edilen konsolun dikey senkronizasyonu. Açık: oyunlar saniyede en fazla 60 kare gösterir, böylece 60 FPS yaması olan oyunlar hızlanmaz. Kapalı: sınır yok ve bazı oyunlar fazla hızlı çalışır.
How the game's picture looks and how smoothly it is shown: image filter, anti-aliasing, colors, frame rate limit, frame generation and the rate sent to your TV. The game itself runs the same. = Oyunun görüntüsünün nasıl göründüğü ve ne kadar akıcı gösterildiği: görüntü filtresi, kenar yumuşatma, renkler, kare sınırı, kare üretimi ve TV'ye gönderilen hız. Oyunun kendisi aynı çalışır.
How the emulated Xbox 360 runs each game: its video rate and resolution, VSync, the renderer, memory, readbacks, shaders and tests. They can change a game's speed or correctness; change them game by game. = Emüle edilen Xbox 360'ın her oyunu nasıl çalıştırdığı: görüntü hızı ve çözünürlük, VSync, görüntü işleyici, bellek, geri okumalar, gölgelendiriciler ve denemeler. Bir oyunun hızını veya doğruluğunu değiştirebilirler; oyun oyun değiştirin.
Puts every option of the Picture and smoothness, Audio, Controls, System and Emulation (advanced) pages back to its recommended value. Each game's own settings stay as they are. = Görüntü ve akıcılık, Ses, Kontroller, Sistem ve Emülasyon (gelişmiş) sayfalarındaki her seçeneği önerilen değerine döndürür. Her oyunun kendi ayarları olduğu gibi kalır.
Anti-aliasing and sharpness = Kenar yumuşatma ve keskinlik
Colors and screen fit = Renkler ve ekrana sığdırma

Each identified game's own settings, in the same pages as these. What a game does not change follows these general settings. = Tanınan her oyunun kendi ayarları, bunlarla aynı sayfalarda. Bir oyunun değiştirmediği şey bu genel ayarları izler.
Covers, game folders, the game list and saved data. = Kapaklar, oyun klasörleri, oyun listesi ve kayıtlı veriler.
Each game can have its own picture, audio, controls, system and emulation settings. What it does not change follows the general settings. In the library, Triangle on a game opens the same settings. = Her oyunun kendi görüntü, ses, kontrol, sistem ve emülasyon ayarları olabilir. Değiştirmediği şey genel ayarları izler. Kitaplıkta bir oyunun üzerinde Üçgen aynı ayarları açar.
Each game can have its own value: Settings > Game settings. = Her oyunun kendi değeri olabilir: Ayarlar > Oyun ayarları.
These options change how the game runs. = Bu seçenekler oyunun çalışmasını değiştirir.
Changed for this game. Too fast or wrong? Set it back. = Bu oyun için değişti. Fazla hızlı veya hatalı mı? Geri alın.
Changes how the game runs: it can run faster, slower or wrong. = Oyunun çalışmasını değiştirir: daha hızlı, daha yavaş veya hatalı çalışabilir.
How the picture is scaled to your screen. CAS sharpens; FSR upscales with cleaner edges. = Görüntünün ekrana nasıl ölçeklendiği. CAS keskinleştirir; FSR daha temiz kenarlarla büyütür.
Smooths jagged edges. FXAA is light; SMAA 1x gives the cleanest edges. = Tırtıklı kenarları yumuşatır. FXAA hafiftir; SMAA 1x en temiz kenarları verir.
Changes the picture's colors: deeper blacks, richer or brighter colors. = Görüntünün renklerini değiştirir: daha derin siyah, daha canlı veya daha açık renkler.
Cuts a thin border off the edges, for games with a stripe at the edge. = Kenarlardan ince bir şerit keser; kenarında şerit görünen oyunlar için.
How strongly CAS and FSR sharpen the picture. = CAS ve FSR'nin görüntüyü ne kadar keskinleştirdiği.
Hides banding in skies and shadows. = Gökyüzü ve gölgelerdeki bantları gizler.
Keep the game's proportions, or stretch the picture to fill the screen. = Oyunun oranlarını koruyun veya görüntüyü tüm ekrana uzatın.
Keeps textures on floors and walls sharp at an angle. = Açılı görülen zemin ve duvar dokularını net tutar.
Shows new pictures at most this often, for even motion. The game runs as before. = Yeni görüntüleri en fazla bu sıklıkta gösterir, düzgün hareket için. Oyun eskisi gibi çalışır.
The emulated console's clock, not your TV. 120 Hz can make games run too fast. = TV'nin değil, emüle edilen konsolun saati. 120 Hz oyunları fazla hızlı çalıştırabilir.
Lighter tracking of data the game rewrites all the time. Off only to compare. = Oyunun sürekli yeniden yazdığı verilerin daha hafif takibi. Yalnızca karşılaştırmak için kapatın.
How much memory is sent to video again after the game writes to it. = Oyun yazdıktan sonra videoya yeniden gönderilen bellek miktarı.
Lets the game read back what it drew. Some effects need it; it is slower. = Oyunun çizdiğini geri okumasını sağlar. Bazı efektler bunu gerektirir; daha yavaştır.
Lets the game read data made by video. Fixes missing objects; it is slower. = Oyunun videonun ürettiği verileri okumasını sağlar. Eksik nesneleri düzeltir; daha yavaştır.
Prepares new effects in the background: fewer stops, effects may appear late. = Yeni efektleri arka planda hazırlar: daha az takılma, efektler geç görünebilir.
A fix for Team Ninja games where characters disappear. It is slower. = Karakterlerin kaybolduğu Team Ninja oyunları için bir düzeltme. Daha yavaştır.
Draws from memory the game already gave back, so nothing goes missing. = Oyunun geri verdiği bellekten de çizer, böylece hiçbir şey eksik kalmaz.
Turns the game's sound off or on. = Oyun sesini kapatır veya açar.
The game's sound volume. The TV's volume still applies. = Oyun sesinin seviyesi. TV'nin ses seviyesi yine geçerlidir.
The emulator's menu sounds. = Emülatörün menü sesleri.
The volume of the menu sounds. = Menü seslerinin seviyesi.
What a touchpad click does: open this guide, or press Xbox Back. = Dokunmatik yüzey tıklamasının ne yaptığı: bu kılavuzu açar veya Xbox Back'e basar.
Sends the game's vibration to the controller. = Oyunun titreşimini kumandaya gönderir.
Ignores small movements of the left stick. = Sol çubuğun küçük hareketlerini yok sayar.
Ignores small movements of the right stick. = Sağ çubuğun küçük hareketlerini yok sayar.
A phone as a motion camera (research). It shows diagnostics. = Telefon, hareket kamerası olarak (araştırma). Tanılama gösterir.
Where the motion panel is drawn over the game. = Hareket panelinin oyunun üzerinde nereye çizildiği.
Sends the phone camera's picture to games that show the camera. = Telefon kamerasının görüntüsünü kamerayı gösteren oyunlara gönderir.
Shows the frames per second in a corner. = Köşede saniyedeki kare sayısını gösterir.
Shows a notification when you unlock an achievement. = Bir başarım açıldığında bildirim gösterir.
Where the game keeps its saves. = Oyunun kayıtlarını tuttuğu yer.

# Fix 148h: an install that stopped (the library's message after the dirty-disc error).
Install data incomplete = Kurulum verileri eksik
%s: its install data is incomplete (%s: %s of %s). In the library press Cross on the game, open the %s tab, select each of its install packages and press Triangle (%s). Then start the game to install it again. = %s: kurulum verileri eksik (%s: %s / %s). Kütüphanede oyunun üzerinde Çarpı'ya basın, "%s" sekmesini açın, her kurulum paketini seçip Üçgen "%s" tuşuna basın. Ardından yeniden kurmak için oyunu başlatın.
The video mode the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game; it is not your TV's resolution. It applies when the game starts. At 1080p or 900p some games draw more pixels (sharper, heavier); at 480p some draw fewer and run lighter. 540p is not a mode a real console reports: the game is told 720p. Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = Emüle edilen Xbox 360'ın oyuna bildirdiği video modu; TV'nin çözünürlüğü değildir. Oyun başladığında uygulanır. 1080p veya 900p'de bazı oyunlar daha fazla piksel çizer (daha keskin, daha ağır); 480p'de bazıları daha az çizer ve daha hafif çalışır. 540p gerçek bir konsolun modu değildir: oyuna 720p bildirilir. Birçok oyun bunu yok sayar ve 720p'de kalır.

# Fix 119: the Kinect colour camera from the phone (camera_picture.hpp).
Camera picture for the game = 供游戏使用的摄像头画面
With a phone as the Kinect, the phone can also send a small picture from its camera for games that show the camera (replays, photos). It goes only to this console, and the emulator keeps it in memory only, never on a drive; a game may still keep photos of its own in its saves. It also needs Camera picture for the game on the phone page. Off, games see a grey picture. = 把手机当作 Kinect 时，手机还可以为显示摄像头画面的游戏（回放、照片）发送一张小的摄像头画面。画面只发送到这台主机，模拟器只把它放在内存中，绝不写入硬盘；不过游戏仍可能在自己的存档中保存照片。还需要在手机页面上打开“供游戏使用的摄像头画面”。关闭时，游戏看到的是灰色画面。
Resolution scale (test) = 渲染分辨率倍数（测试）
Draws the game at twice its own resolution for a sharper picture. Uses much more video memory and graphics power; some effects (glow, shadows, pictures the game reads back) may look wrong. A test: if the game closes or looks broken, go back to 1x. = 以游戏自身分辨率的两倍绘制，画面更清晰。会占用多得多的显存和图形性能；部分效果（光晕、阴影、游戏回读的图像）可能显示错误。这是测试功能：如果游戏关闭或画面异常，请改回 1x。
Resolution scale (test) 2x: this game needs more memory than is left. The picture may break; set it to 1x in this game's settings. = 渲染分辨率倍数（测试）2x：此游戏需要的内存超过剩余内存。画面可能出错；请在此游戏的设置中改为 1x。

# Fix 137: anti-aliasing (FXAA) in the Vulkan swap (launcher option antialiasing).
Anti-aliasing = 抗锯齿
Smooths the jagged edges of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. FXAA: light, keeps text sharp. FXAA Extreme: smoother edges, a slightly softer picture. Vulkan only; it costs little. SMAA 1x: cleaner edges than FXAA with less blur, a little heavier. = 在游戏画面缩放到屏幕之前，平滑其锯齿状边缘。FXAA：轻量，文字保持清晰。FXAA 极致：边缘更平滑，画面略为柔和。仅限 Vulkan，性能开销很小。SMAA 1x：边缘比 FXAA 更干净、模糊更少，开销略高。
# Fix 145: SMAA 1x, the fourth anti-aliasing choice.
SMAA 1x = SMAA 1x
FXAA = FXAA
FXAA Extreme = FXAA 极致
Anti-aliasing works with the Vulkan renderer only. = 抗锯齿仅适用于 Vulkan 渲染器。
# Fix 141: the colour preset and the overscan crop in the Vulkan swap (color_preset, overscan_crop).
Color preset = 色彩预设
Changes the colors of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. OLED Deep Black: deeper blacks, shadow detail kept. Vibrant: richer colors. Clean Neutral: a slightly brighter, clearer picture. Vulkan only. = 在游戏画面缩放到屏幕之前调整其色彩。OLED 深邃黑：黑色更深，同时保留暗部细节。鲜艳：色彩更丰富。清爽中性：画面略微更亮、更通透。仅限 Vulkan。
OLED Deep Black = OLED 深邃黑
Vibrant = 鲜艳
Clean Neutral = 清爽中性
Overscan crop = 过扫描裁切
Cuts a thin border off each edge of the game's picture and enlarges the rest, keeping its proportions: for games that show a stripe or garbage at the edges. Vulkan only. = 从游戏画面的每条边裁掉一圈细边，并在保持比例的同时放大其余部分：适用于边缘出现条纹或杂乱画面的游戏。仅限 Vulkan。
Color presets and overscan crop work with the Vulkan renderer only. = 色彩预设和过扫描裁切仅适用于 Vulkan 渲染器。
900p (1600x900) = 900p (1600x900)
The resolution the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game. At 480p or 540p some games draw fewer pixels and run lighter; at 900p or 1080p some draw more (sharper, heavier). Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = 模拟的 Xbox 360 告知游戏的分辨率。480p 或 540p 时，部分游戏绘制的像素更少、运行更轻；900p 或 1080p 时部分游戏绘制更多（更清晰、更重）。许多游戏会忽略此设置，保持 720p。
Frame rate limit (screen) = 帧率上限（屏幕）
Shows the game's new pictures at most this many times a second, each for the same time, for an even motion on screen. The game itself runs as before (its speed, its sound and the Emulated video rate do not change), and a game slower than the limit is shown as it is. 40 needs the 120 Hz output (Frame generation output: Up to 120 Hz); at 60 Hz it shows every picture. It does nothing while frame generation is on. Automatic: as before. = 每秒最多显示这么多次游戏的新画面，每帧显示相同的时间，让画面运动更均匀。游戏本身照旧运行（速度、声音和模拟的画面刷新率都不变），比上限慢的游戏按原样显示。40 需要 120 Hz 输出（帧生成输出：最高 120 Hz）；在 60 Hz 下会显示每一帧。开启帧生成时不起作用。自动：与以前相同。
No limit = 无限制
# Fix 147c: the Harmonix music keys (overlay and the /keys page).
Music keys needed = 需要音乐密钥
Scan to add the Harmonix music keys (HvKeys). Restart the game after. = 扫描以添加 Harmonix 音乐密钥（HvKeys），然后重新启动游戏。
Where to get them: = 获取位置：
Harmonix music keys = Harmonix 音乐密钥
This game's music is encrypted with Harmonix keys, which are not included. Paste the HvKeys array from keys.h (or one key per line) and save. = 本游戏的音乐使用 Harmonix 密钥加密，密钥未包含在内。请粘贴 keys.h 中的 HvKeys 数组（或每行一个密钥）并保存。
Save = 保存
Keys saved: %u of %u. Restart the game to use them. = 已保存的密钥：%u / %u。重新启动游戏后生效。
Keys refused (no fingerprint matches): %u = 被拒绝的密钥（无匹配的指纹）：%u
Picture and smoothness = 画面与流畅度
Emulation (advanced) = 模拟（高级）
Emulated video rate (game clock, not your TV) = 模拟的画面刷新率（游戏时钟，不是电视）
The emulated Xbox 360's own clock: how many times a second it tells the game a new frame is due. It is not your TV's refresh rate (that is Frame generation output, under Picture and smoothness). At 120 Hz some games held at 30 frames go to 60, but others run too fast. A test, game by game. = 模拟的 Xbox 360 自身的时钟：每秒通知游戏多少次该出新的一帧。它不是电视的刷新率（那是“画面与流畅度”中的“帧生成输出”）。在 120 Hz 下，一些锁在 30 帧的游戏会变成 60 帧，但另一些游戏会运行过快。这是逐个游戏的测试。
VSync (emulated, game speed) = VSync（模拟，游戏速度）
The emulated console's vertical sync, not your TV's. On: games show at most 60 frames a second, so games with a 60 FPS patch do not speed up. Off: no cap, and some games run too fast. = 模拟主机的垂直同步，不是电视的。开：游戏每秒最多显示 60 帧，因此打了 60 FPS 补丁的游戏不会加速。关：没有上限，一些游戏会运行过快。
How the game's picture looks and how smoothly it is shown: image filter, anti-aliasing, colors, frame rate limit, frame generation and the rate sent to your TV. The game itself runs the same. = 游戏画面的样子以及显示的流畅程度：图像滤镜、抗锯齿、颜色、帧率上限、帧生成以及发送到电视的刷新率。游戏本身的运行不变。
How the emulated Xbox 360 runs each game: its video rate and resolution, VSync, the renderer, memory, readbacks, shaders and tests. They can change a game's speed or correctness; change them game by game. = 模拟的 Xbox 360 如何运行每个游戏：画面刷新率和分辨率、VSync、渲染器、内存、回读、着色器和测试。它们可能改变游戏的速度或正确性；请逐个游戏调整。
Puts every option of the Picture and smoothness, Audio, Controls, System and Emulation (advanced) pages back to its recommended value. Each game's own settings stay as they are. = 将“画面与流畅度”、“音频”、“控制”、“系统”和“模拟（高级）”页面中的每个选项恢复为推荐值。每个游戏自己的设置保持不变。
Anti-aliasing and sharpness = 抗锯齿与锐度
Colors and screen fit = 颜色与画面适配
Frame rate and 120 Hz = 帧率与 120 Hz
Detail and resolution = 细节与分辨率
Video clock and mode = 画面时钟与模式
Renderer and accuracy = 渲染器与准确度
Video memory and readback = 显存与回读
Shaders and threads = 着色器与线程
Processor = 处理器
Library and saves = 游戏库与存档
Each identified game's own settings, in the same pages as these. What a game does not change follows these general settings. = 每个已识别游戏自己的设置，页面与这里相同。游戏未更改的项目沿用这些通用设置。
Covers, game folders, the game list and saved data. = 封面、游戏文件夹、游戏列表和存档数据。
Each game can have its own picture, audio, controls, system and emulation settings. What it does not change follows the general settings. In the library, Triangle on a game opens the same settings. = 每个游戏都可以有自己的画面、音频、控制、系统和模拟设置。未更改的项目沿用通用设置。在游戏库中对游戏按三角键会打开同样的设置。
Each game can have its own value: Settings > Game settings. = 每个游戏都可以有自己的值：设置 > 游戏设置。
These options change how the game runs. = 这些选项会改变游戏的运行方式。
Changed for this game. Too fast or wrong? Set it back. = 已为此游戏更改。过快或出错？请改回。
Changes how the game runs: it can run faster, slower or wrong. = 改变游戏的运行方式：可能变快、变慢或出错。
How the picture is scaled to your screen. CAS sharpens; FSR upscales with cleaner edges. = 画面如何缩放到屏幕。CAS 锐化；FSR 放大并让边缘更干净。
Smooths jagged edges. FXAA is light; SMAA 1x gives the cleanest edges. = 平滑锯齿边缘。FXAA 较轻；SMAA 1x 边缘最干净。
Changes the picture's colors: deeper blacks, richer or brighter colors. = 改变画面颜色：更深的黑色、更鲜艳或更明亮的颜色。
Cuts a thin border off the edges, for games with a stripe at the edge. = 裁掉边缘的一条细边，适用于边缘有条纹的游戏。
How strongly CAS and FSR sharpen the picture. = CAS 和 FSR 锐化画面的强度。
Hides banding in skies and shadows. = 隐藏天空和阴影中的色带。
Keep the game's proportions, or stretch the picture to fill the screen. = 保持游戏的比例，或拉伸画面填满屏幕。
Keeps textures on floors and walls sharp at an angle. = 让斜看时地面和墙面的纹理保持清晰。
Shows new pictures at most this often, for even motion. The game runs as before. = 新画面最多以此频率显示，让运动更均匀。游戏照旧运行。
The emulated console's clock, not your TV. 120 Hz can make games run too fast. = 模拟主机的时钟，不是电视的。120 Hz 可能让游戏运行过快。
Lighter tracking of data the game rewrites all the time. Off only to compare. = 更轻量地跟踪游戏不断重写的数据。仅在对比时关闭。
How much memory is sent to video again after the game writes to it. = 游戏写入后重新发送给视频的内存量。
Lets the game read back what it drew. Some effects need it; it is slower. = 让游戏读回它绘制的内容。部分效果需要；会更慢。
Lets the game read data made by video. Fixes missing objects; it is slower. = 让游戏读取视频生成的数据。修复缺失的物体；会更慢。
Prepares new effects in the background: fewer stops, effects may appear late. = 在后台准备新效果：卡顿更少，效果可能稍后出现。
A fix for Team Ninja games where characters disappear. It is slower. = 针对角色消失的 Team Ninja 游戏的修正。会更慢。
Draws from memory the game already gave back, so nothing goes missing. = 也从游戏已释放的内存绘制，避免内容缺失。
Turns the game's sound off or on. = 关闭或打开游戏声音。
The game's sound volume. The TV's volume still applies. = 游戏声音的音量。电视音量仍然有效。
The emulator's menu sounds. = 模拟器的菜单音效。
The volume of the menu sounds. = 菜单音效的音量。
What a touchpad click does: open this guide, or press Xbox Back. = 触摸板点击的作用：打开此菜单，或按下 Xbox Back。
Sends the game's vibration to the controller. = 将游戏的振动发送到手柄。
Ignores small movements of the left stick. = 忽略左摇杆的小幅移动。
Ignores small movements of the right stick. = 忽略右摇杆的小幅移动。
A phone as a motion camera (research). It shows diagnostics. = 把手机用作动作摄像头（研究）。显示诊断信息。
Where the motion panel is drawn over the game. = 动作面板在游戏上方的位置。
Sends the phone camera's picture to games that show the camera. = 把手机摄像头画面发送给显示摄像头的游戏。
Shows the frames per second in a corner. = 在角落显示每秒帧数。
Shows a notification when you unlock an achievement. = 解锁成就时显示通知。
Where the game keeps its saves. = 游戏存放存档的位置。

# Fix 148h: an install that stopped (the library's message after the dirty-disc error).
Install data incomplete = 安装数据不完整
%s: its install data is incomplete (%s: %s of %s). In the library press Cross on the game, open the %s tab, select each of its install packages and press Triangle (%s). Then start the game to install it again. = %s：安装数据不完整（%s：%s / %s）。在游戏库中对该游戏按叉键，打开“%s”标签，选择每个安装包并按三角键“%s”。然后启动游戏以重新安装。
The video mode the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game; it is not your TV's resolution. It applies when the game starts. At 1080p or 900p some games draw more pixels (sharper, heavier); at 480p some draw fewer and run lighter. 540p is not a mode a real console reports: the game is told 720p. Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = 模拟的 Xbox 360 告诉游戏的视频模式；不是电视的分辨率。在游戏启动时生效。在 1080p 或 900p 下，一些游戏绘制更多像素（更清晰，更吃性能）；在 480p 下，一些游戏绘制更少，运行更轻松。540p 不是真实主机的模式：游戏得到的是 720p。许多游戏会忽略它，保持在 720p。

# Fix 107: the emergency pose and the re-centre from the pad (motion_emergency.hpp).
No player seen. Hold L3 + R3 to bring the last pose back, or press them briefly to re-centre the phone camera from here. Stand 2.5-3 m from the phone with your hips in view. = 未偵測到玩家。按住 L3 + R3 可恢復上一個姿勢，短按則可在這裡重新置中手機相機。請站在離手機 2.5-3 公尺處，讓臀部進入畫面。
Emergency pose on: the game sees the last pose. Let go of L3 + R3 to stop; a body from the phone takes over. = 緊急姿勢已開啟：遊戲看到的是上一個姿勢。放開 L3 + R3 即可停止；手機偵測到身體後會接手。
Re-centring the phone camera in %d... Stand still at your spot. = %d 秒後重新置中手機相機... 請站在原地不動。
Re-centring the phone camera... Stand still at your spot. = 正在重新置中手機相機... 請站在原地不動。

It turns itself off after that game. = 在你下次啟動的遊戲中，模擬器會更詳細地記錄發生的情況。玩到問題出現，然後關閉遊戲。之後會有一個畫面列出要附加到錯誤回報中的檔案。該遊戲結束後此選項會自動關閉。

This game's next start records more details. Afterwards a screen lists the files to attach to your bug report. = 這個遊戲下次啟動時會記錄更多細節。之後會有一個畫面列出要附加到錯誤回報中的檔案。
Restart this game with logs for a bug report = 以錯誤回報記錄重新啟動此遊戲
This session did not end normally: the game stopped or the emulator was closed. It began on = 此工作階段未正常結束：遊戲停止了，或模擬器被關閉。開始於
Saving shaders for faster loading next time... = 正在儲存著色器，以便下次載入更快...

Signing out of online play... = 正在登出線上遊戲...
Saving the logs for the bug report... = 正在儲存錯誤回報記錄...
Starting the menu... = 正在啟動選單...
Starting the game... = 正在啟動遊戲...
Tip: leave with Exit emulator rather than the PS button, so the shaders are kept for next time. = 提示：請使用「結束模擬器」而不是 PS 按鈕離開，以便保留著色器供下次使用。

# Fix 119: the Kinect colour camera from the phone (camera_picture.hpp).
Camera picture for the game = 供遊戲使用的攝影機畫面
With a phone as the Kinect, the phone can also send a small picture from its camera for games that show the camera (replays, photos). It goes only to this console, and the emulator keeps it in memory only, never on a drive; a game may still keep photos of its own in its saves. It also needs Camera picture for the game on the phone page. Off, games see a grey picture. = 把手機當作 Kinect 時，手機還可以為顯示攝影機畫面的遊戲（重播、照片）傳送一張小的攝影機畫面。畫面只傳送到這台主機，模擬器只把它放在記憶體中，絕不寫入硬碟；不過遊戲仍可能在自己的存檔中保存照片。還需要在手機頁面上開啟「供遊戲使用的攝影機畫面」。關閉時，遊戲看到的是灰色畫面。
Resolution scale (test) = 渲染解析度倍數（測試）
Draws the game at twice its own resolution for a sharper picture. Uses much more video memory and graphics power; some effects (glow, shadows, pictures the game reads back) may look wrong. A test: if the game closes or looks broken, go back to 1x. = 以遊戲本身解析度的兩倍繪製，畫面更清晰。會佔用多得多的顯示記憶體和圖形效能；部分效果（光暈、陰影、遊戲回讀的影像）可能顯示錯誤。這是測試功能：如果遊戲關閉或畫面異常，請改回 1x。
Resolution scale (test) 2x: this game needs more memory than is left. The picture may break; set it to 1x in this game's settings. = 渲染解析度倍數（測試）2x：此遊戲需要的記憶體超過剩餘記憶體。畫面可能出錯；請在此遊戲的設定中改為 1x。

Avatar = 虛擬人偶
Avatar saved. Games that show avatars use it from their next start. = 虛擬人偶已儲存。顯示虛擬人偶的遊戲會從下次啟動起使用它。
Could not save the avatar. = 無法儲存虛擬人偶。
The body shape of this profile's avatar, for games that show the player as an avatar (Kinect Sports). Only the shape for now: the avatar has no clothes or face yet. = 此設定檔的虛擬人偶體型，用於以虛擬人偶顯示玩家的遊戲（Kinect Sports）。目前只有體型：虛擬人偶還沒有衣服和臉。
Body type = 體型
Female = 女性
Male = 男性
The avatar's build: a female body has slightly wider hips and narrower shoulders. = 虛擬人偶的體格：女性身體的臀部稍寬，肩膀較窄。
Height = 身高
Shorter or taller limbs and body. = 更短或更長的四肢和軀幹。
Weight = 體重
A thinner or heavier build. = 更瘦或更壯的體格。
# Fix 137: anti-aliasing (FXAA) in the Vulkan swap (launcher option antialiasing).
Anti-aliasing = 反鋸齒
Smooths the jagged edges of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. FXAA: light, keeps text sharp. FXAA Extreme: smoother edges, a slightly softer picture. Vulkan only; it costs little. SMAA 1x: cleaner edges than FXAA with less blur, a little heavier. = 在遊戲畫面縮放至螢幕之前，平滑其鋸齒狀邊緣。FXAA：輕量，文字保持清晰。FXAA 極致：邊緣更平滑，畫面略為柔和。僅限 Vulkan，效能負擔很小。SMAA 1x：邊緣比 FXAA 更乾淨、模糊更少，負擔略高。
# Fix 145: SMAA 1x, the fourth anti-aliasing choice.
SMAA 1x = SMAA 1x
FXAA = FXAA
FXAA Extreme = FXAA 極致
Anti-aliasing works with the Vulkan renderer only. = 反鋸齒僅適用於 Vulkan 繪圖器。
# Fix 141: the colour preset and the overscan crop in the Vulkan swap (color_preset, overscan_crop).
Color preset = 色彩預設
Changes the colors of the game's picture before it is scaled to the screen. OLED Deep Black: deeper blacks, shadow detail kept. Vibrant: richer colors. Clean Neutral: a slightly brighter, clearer picture. Vulkan only. = 在遊戲畫面縮放至螢幕之前調整其色彩。OLED 深邃黑：黑色更深，同時保留暗部細節。鮮豔：色彩更豐富。清爽中性：畫面略為更亮、更通透。僅限 Vulkan。
OLED Deep Black = OLED 深邃黑
Vibrant = 鮮豔
Clean Neutral = 清爽中性
Overscan crop = 過掃描裁切
Cuts a thin border off each edge of the game's picture and enlarges the rest, keeping its proportions: for games that show a stripe or garbage at the edges. Vulkan only. = 從遊戲畫面的每條邊裁掉一圈細邊，並在保持比例的同時放大其餘部分：適用於邊緣出現條紋或雜亂畫面的遊戲。僅限 Vulkan。
Color presets and overscan crop work with the Vulkan renderer only. = 色彩預設和過掃描裁切僅適用於 Vulkan 繪圖器。
900p (1600x900) = 900p (1600x900)
The resolution the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game. At 480p or 540p some games draw fewer pixels and run lighter; at 900p or 1080p some draw more (sharper, heavier). Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = 模擬的 Xbox 360 告知遊戲的解析度。480p 或 540p 時，部分遊戲繪製的像素更少、執行更輕；900p 或 1080p 時部分遊戲繪製更多（更清晰、更重）。許多遊戲會忽略此設定，保持 720p。
Frame rate limit (screen) = 影格率上限（螢幕）
Shows the game's new pictures at most this many times a second, each for the same time, for an even motion on screen. The game itself runs as before (its speed, its sound and the Emulated video rate do not change), and a game slower than the limit is shown as it is. 40 needs the 120 Hz output (Frame generation output: Up to 120 Hz); at 60 Hz it shows every picture. It does nothing while frame generation is on. Automatic: as before. = 每秒最多顯示這麼多次遊戲的新畫面，每個畫面顯示相同的時間，讓畫面動作更平順。遊戲本身照常執行（速度、聲音和模擬的畫面更新率都不變），比上限慢的遊戲會照原樣顯示。40 需要 120 Hz 輸出（幀生成輸出：最高 120 Hz）；在 60 Hz 下會顯示每個畫面。開啟幀生成時不起作用。自動：與以前相同。
No limit = 無限制
# Fix 147c: the Harmonix music keys (overlay and the /keys page).
Music keys needed = 需要音樂金鑰
Scan to add the Harmonix music keys (HvKeys). Restart the game after. = 掃描以新增 Harmonix 音樂金鑰（HvKeys），然後重新啟動遊戲。
Where to get them: = 取得位置：
Harmonix music keys = Harmonix 音樂金鑰
This game's music is encrypted with Harmonix keys, which are not included. Paste the HvKeys array from keys.h (or one key per line) and save. = 本遊戲的音樂使用 Harmonix 金鑰加密，金鑰並未包含在內。請貼上 keys.h 中的 HvKeys 陣列（或每行一個金鑰）並儲存。
Save = 儲存
Keys saved: %u of %u. Restart the game to use them. = 已儲存的金鑰：%u / %u。重新啟動遊戲後生效。
Keys refused (no fingerprint matches): %u = 遭拒絕的金鑰（沒有相符的指紋）：%u
Picture and smoothness = 畫面與流暢度
Emulation (advanced) = 模擬（進階）
Emulated video rate (game clock, not your TV) = 模擬的畫面更新率（遊戲時脈，不是電視）
The emulated Xbox 360's own clock: how many times a second it tells the game a new frame is due. It is not your TV's refresh rate (that is Frame generation output, under Picture and smoothness). At 120 Hz some games held at 30 frames go to 60, but others run too fast. A test, game by game. = 模擬的 Xbox 360 本身的時脈：每秒通知遊戲幾次該出新的一格畫面。它不是電視的更新率（那是「畫面與流暢度」中的「幀生成輸出」）。在 120 Hz 下，一些鎖在 30 幀的遊戲會變成 60 幀，但另一些遊戲會執行過快。這是逐一遊戲的測試。
VSync (emulated, game speed) = VSync（模擬，遊戲速度）
The emulated console's vertical sync, not your TV's. On: games show at most 60 frames a second, so games with a 60 FPS patch do not speed up. Off: no cap, and some games run too fast. = 模擬主機的垂直同步，不是電視的。開：遊戲每秒最多顯示 60 幀，因此套用 60 FPS 修補的遊戲不會加速。關：沒有上限，一些遊戲會執行過快。
How the game's picture looks and how smoothly it is shown: image filter, anti-aliasing, colors, frame rate limit, frame generation and the rate sent to your TV. The game itself runs the same. = 遊戲畫面的樣子以及顯示的流暢程度：影像濾鏡、反鋸齒、色彩、影格率上限、幀生成以及傳送到電視的更新率。遊戲本身的執行不變。
How the emulated Xbox 360 runs each game: its video rate and resolution, VSync, the renderer, memory, readbacks, shaders and tests. They can change a game's speed or correctness; change them game by game. = 模擬的 Xbox 360 如何執行每個遊戲：畫面更新率與解析度、VSync、渲染器、記憶體、回讀、著色器和測試。它們可能改變遊戲的速度或正確性；請逐一遊戲調整。
Puts every option of the Picture and smoothness, Audio, Controls, System and Emulation (advanced) pages back to its recommended value. Each game's own settings stay as they are. = 將「畫面與流暢度」、「音訊」、「控制」、「系統」和「模擬（進階）」頁面中的每個選項恢復為建議值。每個遊戲自己的設定維持不變。
Anti-aliasing and sharpness = 反鋸齒與銳利度
Colors and screen fit = 色彩與畫面調整
Frame rate and 120 Hz = 影格率與 120 Hz
Detail and resolution = 細節與解析度
Video clock and mode = 畫面時脈與模式
Renderer and accuracy = 渲染器與準確度
Video memory and readback = 視訊記憶體與回讀
Shaders and threads = 著色器與執行緒
Processor = 處理器
Library and saves = 遊戲庫與存檔
Each identified game's own settings, in the same pages as these. What a game does not change follows these general settings. = 每個已識別遊戲自己的設定，頁面與這裡相同。遊戲未變更的項目沿用這些一般設定。
Covers, game folders, the game list and saved data. = 封面、遊戲資料夾、遊戲清單和存檔資料。
Each game can have its own picture, audio, controls, system and emulation settings. What it does not change follows the general settings. In the library, Triangle on a game opens the same settings. = 每個遊戲都可以有自己的畫面、音訊、控制、系統和模擬設定。未變更的項目沿用一般設定。在遊戲庫中對遊戲按三角鍵會開啟同樣的設定。
Each game can have its own value: Settings > Game settings. = 每個遊戲都可以有自己的值：設定 > 遊戲設定。
These options change how the game runs. = 這些選項會改變遊戲的執行方式。
Changed for this game. Too fast or wrong? Set it back. = 已為此遊戲變更。過快或出錯？請改回。
Changes how the game runs: it can run faster, slower or wrong. = 改變遊戲的執行方式：可能變快、變慢或出錯。
How the picture is scaled to your screen. CAS sharpens; FSR upscales with cleaner edges. = 畫面如何縮放到螢幕。CAS 銳化；FSR 放大並讓邊緣更乾淨。
Smooths jagged edges. FXAA is light; SMAA 1x gives the cleanest edges. = 平滑鋸齒邊緣。FXAA 較輕；SMAA 1x 邊緣最乾淨。
Changes the picture's colors: deeper blacks, richer or brighter colors. = 改變畫面色彩：更深的黑色、更鮮豔或更明亮的色彩。
Cuts a thin border off the edges, for games with a stripe at the edge. = 裁掉邊緣的一條細邊，適用於邊緣有條紋的遊戲。
How strongly CAS and FSR sharpen the picture. = CAS 和 FSR 銳化畫面的強度。
Hides banding in skies and shadows. = 隱藏天空和陰影中的色帶。
Keep the game's proportions, or stretch the picture to fill the screen. = 保持遊戲的比例，或拉伸畫面填滿螢幕。
Keeps textures on floors and walls sharp at an angle. = 讓斜看時地面和牆面的紋理保持清晰。
Shows new pictures at most this often, for even motion. The game runs as before. = 新畫面最多以此頻率顯示，讓動作更平順。遊戲照常執行。
The emulated console's clock, not your TV. 120 Hz can make games run too fast. = 模擬主機的時脈，不是電視的。120 Hz 可能讓遊戲執行過快。
Lighter tracking of data the game rewrites all the time. Off only to compare. = 更輕量地追蹤遊戲不斷重寫的資料。僅在比較時關閉。
How much memory is sent to video again after the game writes to it. = 遊戲寫入後重新傳送給視訊的記憶體量。
Lets the game read back what it drew. Some effects need it; it is slower. = 讓遊戲讀回它繪製的內容。部分效果需要；會更慢。
Lets the game read data made by video. Fixes missing objects; it is slower. = 讓遊戲讀取視訊產生的資料。修正缺少的物件；會更慢。
Prepares new effects in the background: fewer stops, effects may appear late. = 在背景準備新效果：卡頓更少，效果可能稍後出現。
A fix for Team Ninja games where characters disappear. It is slower. = 針對角色消失的 Team Ninja 遊戲的修正。會更慢。
Draws from memory the game already gave back, so nothing goes missing. = 也從遊戲已釋放的記憶體繪製，避免內容缺少。
Turns the game's sound off or on. = 關閉或開啟遊戲聲音。
The game's sound volume. The TV's volume still applies. = 遊戲聲音的音量。電視音量仍然有效。
The emulator's menu sounds. = 模擬器的選單音效。
The volume of the menu sounds. = 選單音效的音量。
What a touchpad click does: open this guide, or press Xbox Back. = 觸控板點擊的作用：開啟此選單，或按下 Xbox Back。
Sends the game's vibration to the controller. = 將遊戲的震動傳送到控制器。
Ignores small movements of the left stick. = 忽略左搖桿的小幅移動。
Ignores small movements of the right stick. = 忽略右搖桿的小幅移動。
A phone as a motion camera (research). It shows diagnostics. = 把手機當作動作攝影機（研究）。顯示診斷資訊。
Where the motion panel is drawn over the game. = 動作面板在遊戲上方的位置。
Sends the phone camera's picture to games that show the camera. = 把手機攝影機畫面傳送給顯示攝影機的遊戲。
Shows the frames per second in a corner. = 在角落顯示每秒影格數。
Shows a notification when you unlock an achievement. = 解鎖成就時顯示通知。
Where the game keeps its saves. = 遊戲存放存檔的位置。
# Fix 148f: a package shorter than its header says (the DLCs tab).

# Fix 148h: an install that stopped (the library's message after the dirty-disc error).
Install data incomplete = 安裝資料不完整
%s: its install data is incomplete (%s: %s of %s). In the library press Cross on the game, open the %s tab, select each of its install packages and press Triangle (%s). Then start the game to install it again. = %s：安裝資料不完整（%s：%s / %s）。在遊戲庫中對該遊戲按叉鍵，開啟「%s」分頁，選擇每個安裝套件並按三角鍵「%s」。然後啟動遊戲以重新安裝。
The video mode the emulated Xbox 360 reports to the game; it is not your TV's resolution. It applies when the game starts. At 1080p or 900p some games draw more pixels (sharper, heavier); at 480p some draw fewer and run lighter. 540p is not a mode a real console reports: the game is told 720p. Many games ignore it and stay at 720p. = 模擬的 Xbox 360 告訴遊戲的視訊模式；不是電視的解析度。在遊戲啟動時生效。在 1080p 或 900p 下，一些遊戲繪製更多像素（更清晰、更吃效能）；在 480p 下，一些遊戲繪製較少，執行更輕鬆。540p 不是真實主機的模式：遊戲得到的是 720p。許多遊戲會忽略它，維持在 720p。
